DAVID-TENSOR24-THE-CHARGE_CHECKS.txt: the checks of DAVID-TENSOR24-THE-CHARGE.md (the charge Q* the one formula keeps).
A helper's probes (common.py and rewrites.py the shared code: the head imported read-only and patched in memory, QR1 to QR4
as flags), rerun by David on toys-5 with its own src, all eighteen runs byte for byte identical to the helper's. Each run is
venv314/bin/python -I <probe> <arguments>, from the probes' folder. Then the helper's draft with its proofs in full, and the
sources. Exact integers and rationals; sizes in VSU (Q* times Gamma^2 / T), where a fitted hand carries e = 18,000.

== venv314/bin/python -I c2_head.py world_bang.json 12 ==

  QR1 + QR2 + QR3 + QR4                   : 30 ticks forward, 30 back: start state returned bit for bit: True; turned clock-ticks 746, births 4 (unspringings 1); the run moved the state: True
    (mutant: QR2 spelt with no involution: the rewind raised ValueError)
  mutant: QR2 spelt with no involution    : 30 ticks forward, 30 back: start state returned bit for bit: False; turned clock-ticks 1016, births 25 (unspringings 21); the run moved the state: True
  mutant: QR4 read forward only           : 30 ticks forward, 30 back: start state returned bit for bit: False; turned clock-ticks 739, births 3 (unspringings 1); the run moved the state: True
  every variant rewinds: True; the mutants fail to rewind: True

== the draft (DERIVATION.md) ==
# The charge the one formula keeps: Q*, its two breaks, what they leave, and the clicks

A helper's draft for David (the derivations' owner), 2026-10-28, answering the Boss's findings of #2187 comment
6468334029 (draft A's verifier: the step keeps Q*, the sweep weighed by the pawl's sign and the mesh rate; two breaks
stand, the triple turn summed with no pawl and the turnover flipping the pawl with the stored sweep kept). Read on
toys-5, whose src is engine-5.0-test's step (cut 0's letter, DAVID-TENSOR24-THE-ONE-FORMULA.md). No file of the repo is
changed: every probe imports the head with `venv314/bin/python -I` and patches it in memory (rewrites.py). Every claim
is marked PROVED (with its proof), CHECKED (with its count) or OPEN. Sizes are in VSU, Q* times Gamma^2 / T: a fitted
hand (sigma A, i B) at the vacuum carries |Q*| = A B = e = 18,000 VSU. The charge's rewrites are named QR1 to QR4,
so that the fakes' R1 to R14 keep their labels.

**Verdict.**
- The one formula keeps, tick by tick, the charge Q*_t = SUM_x d(x) / (2 X p^2):
  - d is the stride crossed with the stride's midpoint, weighed by the pawl's sign;
  - the weight is 1/(X p^2), the tick's own stretch and mesh rate;
  - in the rationals it is kept exactly wherever the Mesh's current is antisymmetric;
  - in integers it is kept to Rule3's torque, exactly booked (PROVED, CHECKED bit for bit).
- QR1 (the triple summed through the pawl) and QR2 (the turnover turning the stored sweep with the pawl) remove the two
  named breaks exactly, and both keep the rewind (PROVED, CHECKED).
- They do not give "the charge's conservation derived". Three more terms stand, each exact:
  - the step a split top discards (QR4 removes it);
  - the weights' move between ticks (QR3 removes it);
  - the clicks (OPEN).
- With QR1 to QR4 the step and the turnover move Q* by Rule3's torque and the turn's rounding alone. The clicks still do
  not keep it.

## 0. The words and the integers

- **The hand.** At a Node-clock x a hand is now a = three + i twelve, before a-. Its fine teeth r, one per gear, sit at
  the stride's teeth W, the hand gear's teeth, the regulator's mean teeth (T div 2)(X- + X); mid W = W div 2.
- **Its readings.** rho = r - mid W (the two gears' offsets as one Gaussian integer); v = a - a-.
- **The stride.** P = W v + rho, the step's own momentum.
- **The sweep.** S = Im(conj(a) a-) = three twelve- - twelve three- (hands.sweep).
- **The pawl's sign.** eps(x) = 1 - b - b, b the ratchet's fine tooth (0 or 1, complemented at every turnover of x).
  The Mesh's pawl is f = eps(x) eps(y) (meshes.pawl).
- **The step's two numbers.** X is the stretch now, the impulse's s. p is the mesh rate at x's own load (readings.rates_of).
- **The grip.** g_j = 2 p^2 Q_j pinion. S_j = g_j / p^2 = 2 Q_j pinion is the same from both ends, since Q_j reads the
  two ends' tensions (CHECKED, C3b).
- **The turned neighbour.** m_j(x) is the neighbour's hand across spoke j (clock.offset_pull):
  - seen through the pawl;
  - turned by the offset's pair (c, s)(n) over the standard length L, at the count n = K_x - f K_y;
  - turned by the triple t = g^2 / |g|^2, with g = (4 Gamma w2 - odd m) + i (4 Gamma m + odd w2), where odd is the odd
    gear's sum on the Mesh and m the flow gears' skew.
- **The pull.** F = SUM_j g_j (m_j - a) - D a.
- **The step (law 2 on a hand).** P+ = P + X F; a+ = a + (P+ + mid W+) div W+; r+ = (P+ + mid W+) mod W+.
  So P+ = W+ v+ + rho+, with v+ = a+ - a.
- **Rule3's torque.** tau = Im(conj(v) rho), the notch step crossed with the fine teeth's offset.

## 1. The step's continuity at a Node-clock

**Three densities** of one hand at one clock:

    A   eps W S                                          the notches alone
    B   -eps Im(conj(a) P)       = eps (W S + Im(conj(rho) a))              the verifier's
    d   -eps Im(conj(a + a-) P)  = eps (W S + W S + Im(conj(rho) (a + a-)))  the stride's midpoint, doubled

**Their step**, exactly:

    dA = -eps X Im(conj a F) + eps (Im(conj rho a) - Im(conj rho+ a))
    dB = -eps X Im(conj a F) - eps tau+
    dd = -eps (X Im(conj a F) + X Im(conj a F)) - eps (tau + tau+)

**PROVED.**
- P+ = W+ v+ + rho+ and Im(conj(v+) v+) = 0, so Im(conj(a+) P+) = Im(conj(a) P+) + tau+.
- Im(conj(a) P+) = Im(conj(a) P) + X Im(conj(a) F).
- For d: conj(a+ + a) = conj(a + a) + conj(v+), and conj(a + a-) = conj(a + a) - conj(v).

**CHECKED.**
- c1 A0 to A4: 60,000 random states each, 0 off (both teeth parities, to 200 bits).
- c2 C1A, C1B, C1C, at every live clock of every hand-tick, 0 off:
  - the head: 456 hand-ticks (hole 180, nature 180, polarization 60, bang 36);
  - all 14 runs together: 2,016 hand-ticks.
- This is the readings probe's continuity (208 of 208) in all three forms.
- Its mutants fail:
  - the remainders' terms dropped (c1 A1-m1);
  - rho+ centred on the start's teeth (A1-m2);
  - the torque dropped (A2-m3; on the engine M1: 138 of 180 hand-ticks fail on the hole, 150 on nature);
  - the impulse's sign flipped (A1-m4).

**The density to use is d (form C).**
- Its torque term is the smallest on the head. Hole, per tick: form A up to 28,212 VSU, B 8,503, C 6,646.
- It is odd under the exchange of the two slots. So the rewritten turnover keeps it exactly (section 4b).
- Below, Q* means SUM_x lam(x) d(x) / 2. Form A and the verifier's B obey the same books with their own torque terms.

## 2. The flux across a Mesh, and when it is antisymmetric

**The Im part of the pull is the Meshes' alone.** X Im(conj a F) = X p^2 SUM_j S_j Im(conj(a) m_j): the hold's and the
spring's terms are real multiples of a.
- PROVED.
- CHECKED: c2 C3, 456 of 456 head hand-ticks. The pull is rebuilt bit for bit from the engine's own m_j (C3a).

**The exact flux out of x through spoke j** (the one formula's letter with the turn unrounded):

    J_j(x) = eps(x) S_j(x) Im( conj(a(x)) U_j(x) K^f a(x + e_j) ),    U = o t,  o = (c + i s)(n) / L,  t = g^2 / |g|^2

x's charge falls by the sum of its six outflows (section 3 gives the weight).

**Antisymmetry.** J_-j(x + e_j) = -J_j(x) for every state, if and only if

    Re U_yx = Re U_xy   and   Im U_yx = -f Im U_xy.

- PROVED.
  - Where f = +1: conj(a_y) conj(U) a_x = conj(conj(a_x) U a_y).
  - Where f = -1: the two terms are eps_x Im(U_xy conj(a_x a_y)) and -eps_x Im(U_yx conj(a_x a_y)); they cancel if and
    only if U_yx = U_xy.
- The same condition makes the force self-adjoint (section 3).

**The offset's pair meets it on every Mesh.** n_yx = -f n_xy; the cosine is even and the sine odd.
- PROVED.
- CHECKED: c1 B5, 40,000 Meshes.

**The head's triple meets it only off the pawl walls.**
- odd_yx = -odd_xy and m_yx = -m_xy, so g_yx = conj g_xy and t_yx = conj t_xy.
- On a wall the defect is exactly

      J_xy + J_yx = 2 eps_x S_xy Im(t_xy) Re( o_xy conj(a_x a_y) ).

- PROVED.
- CHECKED:
  - c1 B2: 19,952 walls, 0 off; 19,952 of 19,952 nonzero;
  - the engine's exact transport against the formula: c2 C5, 456 of 456 hand-ticks;
  - the mutants fail: the count with no pawl (B-m1), the odd gear through the pawl with the skew not (B-m2), the pawl
    sign dropped (B-m3), the grips unweighted by the rates (B-m4).

**The engine rounds U a twice per part.** Its integer flux I_j(x) = eps S_j Im(conj a m_j) is J plus a rounding part,
read exactly.
- CHECKED on the head, by the Mesh defect T = I_xy + I_yx:
  - T is exactly 0 on every Mesh whose turn is the identity;
  - over 164,412 Mesh-ticks (hole 67,500, nature 67,500, polarization 22,500, bang 6,912), the walls are 9,930 (hole),
    11,320 (nature), 3,494 (polarization) and 0 (bang);
  - the largest |T|: 18,405 VSU on a hole wall;
  - the rounding part at most 144 (hole), 12 (nature) and 107 (polarization) VSU per Mesh-tick.

## 3. Noether's identity, and the charge Q*_t

**The symmetry.** R_theta a(x) = e^(i eps(x) theta) a(x): the global phase read through the pawls.
- F(R a) = R F(a) for every theta and every transport, since K e^(i theta) = e^(-i theta) K and f eps_y = eps_x.
- PROVED.
- In integers only the quarter turns i^eps act. CHECKED (c1 C1: 24 random tori, the head and the rewrite alike, 0 off);
  the plain i with no pawl frame fails (C-m4).
- So the head keeps the symmetry. What it breaks is the variational half.

**The variational half.** Let B(a, b) = SUM_x Re(conj(a(x)) F(b)(x)) / p(x)^2, which is SUM lam X Re(conj a F b) with
lam = 1/(X p^2).
- B is symmetric if and only if S_j is the same from both ends and U meets section 2's condition.
- Then the generator i eps is antisymmetric and commutes with F, so B(a, i eps a) = -B(a, i eps a) = 0. This is
  Noether's pairing:

      SUM_x eps(x) lam(x) X(x) Im( conj(a(x)) F(a)(x) ) = 0.

- PROVED.
- CHECKED: c1 C2 and C3, 24 of 24 tori with the triple through the pawl; the head nonzero on 24 of 24 (C4, C5).
- Its mutants fail: lam = 1/p^2 on the impulse, lam = 1/X, and no pawl sign (C-m1 to C-m3).

**The charge.** Sum section 1 with the tick's own weights lam_t = 1/(X_t p_t^2), read at its start:

    Q*_t = SUM_x lam_t(x) d(x) / 2
    Q*_t(after the step) - Q*_t(before) = -SUM_Meshes T  -  SUM_x lam eps (tau + tau+) / 2

- PROVED (section 1 with section 2).
- CHECKED: c2 C6, bit for bit in exact rationals, at 456 of 456 head hand-ticks and at every hand-tick of every run;
  with the pawl sign dropped it fails (M2: 136 of 180 on the hole, 146 on nature).
- In the rationals, with an exact turn that meets the condition, T = 0 and the torque is 0, so Q*_t is kept exactly.
  CHECKED: c4 E1, 6 tori over 14 ticks, kept to the last digit with QR1; with the head's triple it moves at 84 of 84
  ticks; the plain sweep moves (E1-m1).

**The law's weight.**
- At rates fixed in time W = T X, so Q* = T SUM eps S / p^2 plus the fine teeth's term (c4 E1b). That is the law's
  weight 1/p^2 on the sweep, with the pawl's sign added.
- With no pawl sign it is not kept (E1-m2), nor is the stride weighed by 1/p^2 alone (E1-m3).
- The verifier's Q* = -SUM eps Im(conj a P)/(X mr^2) is form B.

**In the three operations.** The per-site book is integer; only the weight divides. Multiplied through by
PI = PROD_x X(x) p(x)^2, the book is integer: SUM_x (PI / (X p^2)) d(x). QR3 (section 5b) makes the weight 1.

## 4. The two breaks, exactly, and their rewrites

### 4a. The triple turn summed with no pawl (QR1)

**Where.** Every pawl wall whose triple is not the identity, that is, where the odd gear or the skew is not 0.

**By how much.** Each such wall adds T_pawl = 2 eps_x S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)) to -SUM T. Per tick on the head: up
to 18,460 VSU on the hole, 6,773 on polarization and 62 on nature.

**QR1, the minimal rewrite.** The odd gear and the skew are summed through the pawl, as the count already is:

    odd_xy = side (A_x + f A_y),      m_xy = 3 K side (V_x + f V_y)

**PROVED.** odd_yx = -f odd_xy and m_yx = -f m_xy, so g_yx = (g3, -f g12) and section 2's condition holds on every Mesh.

**CHECKED.**
- c1 B4 and B6: 40,000 Meshes, 0 off.
- c4 E1: the toy's Q* kept to the last digit.
- On the engine with QR1 the exact transport's defect is 0 at every Mesh-tick. T is the rounding alone, at most 61 VSU
  on the hole's walls. The turnover's reversal stays: up to 1,383,749 VSU a tick on the hole.

**The rewind is kept.** QR1 reads the start's gears alone, the same forward and backward. CHECKED: c3, 30 ticks forward
and back, bit for bit on the hole and nature.

**OPEN: the frame the odd gears hold.** The alternative sum eps_x side (A_x + A_y) meets the condition too; it reads the
potential in the birth frame. The offset is sourced by framed leads, and the turnover does not negate it, so which frame
it holds is open.

### 4b. The turnover flips the pawl and keeps the stored sweep (QR2)

**Where.** Every turned clock. The head exchanges the slots through K, (a, a-) -> (K a-, K a), so:
- S' = S and rho' = -conj(rho), so the stored part Im(conj(a + a-) P) is kept;
- but eps' = -eps, so d' = -d: the turned clock's charge is reversed exactly.

PROVED. CHECKED:
- c1 D2 and D3: 60,000 draws;
- c2 C7a, forms A and C reversed exactly at 6,534 head turned clock-ticks (hole 3,219, nature 2,583, polarization 348,
  bang 384).

**By how much.** -lam d at each turned clock, that is, twice its charge reversed. Per tick: up to 1,370,210 VSU on the
hole (76 e), 1,222,190 on polarization and 52,517 on nature.

**In the birth frame** (Z = K^b z) the head's turnover is the plain exchange (Z, Z-) -> (Z-, Z), which reverses every
charge: the law's own "every charge conjugated (the Wronskian odd under the swap)" (ALGEBRA.md on scribe-5, line 1602).

**QR2, the minimal rewrite.** The turnover exchanges a hand's two slots with no K: hands.signs [1, 1] at clock.reverse,
each fine tooth mirrored once. Then:
- (a, a-) -> (a-, a), S' = -S and P' = -P, so eps' S' = eps S and d' = d;
- the turned clock's stored sweep turns with its pawl;
- in the birth frame the turnover is the antiunitary K on the hand, which keeps the charge.

PROVED. The exactness for d holds off the even-teeth edge, a fine tooth at 0 where the one mirror misses -rho by W (none
met here); form A is exact everywhere.

CHECKED:
- c1 D5 and D6: 60,000 draws;
- on the engine the turnover's own term is 0 at every tick; C7aC holds at every turned clock-tick of every QR2 run;
- the mutant (the pawl read unflipped) fails.

**The rewind is kept.** QR2 is an involution (c1 D1: 60,000 draws). CHECKED: c3, 30 ticks bit for bit on the hole and
nature; QR2 spelt with no involution fails to rewind.

**What QR2 changes (OPEN).**
1. The click's sense test reads plain local sweeps on the start board. QR2 turns the local sweep at a turned clock, so
   two equal framed charges across a wall look conjugate. On nature, in 60 ticks, births go from 3 to 20 and
   unspringings from 0 to 17. The click must read the framed charge (section 6).
2. The law's turnover clause has the run back retrace as "the conjugate hand under the conjugate pairs". Under QR2 the
   hands are turned by K in the birth frame, and the retrace then asks the potentials' phases to turn with them. Not
   checked here.
3. On the twin hands the turnover's conjugation is draft E's T = i sigma_y K. No twin world was probed.

## 5. What QR1 and QR2 leave: four terms, each exact

With QR1 and QR2 the two named terms are 0 at every tick. Q* still moves.

**5a. The step a split top discards.**
- The turned clock ends at its pre-state reversed, its step discarded. Its unturned neighbours keep the flux they
  exchanged with it.
- So dQ* is the flux out of the turned region in that tick, plus its inner Meshes' defects.
- PROVED: c4 E3, 32 of 32.
- CHECKED: c2 C7c and C7d on every run. With QR1 and QR2, up to 133,926 VSU a tick on the hole, 78,055 on polarization and
  36,426 on nature (2 e).
- **QR4 (candidate): the turned clock's Meshes at the teeth for the tick of its turnover.**
  - The trigger X0 <= X1 >= X2 reads the regulator's own notch, and no later act of the tick moves it. So the turned
    clocks are known before the hands' carry.
  - At every clock beside a turned one, the hands' impulse is read with that Mesh's term taken out. The regulator's
    sources keep every Mesh.
  - Backward, the note's turned clocks give the impulse back the same terms.
- PROVED in the toy: E3, kept at 32 of 32 split tops.
- CHECKED on the engine:
  - the turned set read before the carry equals the turnover's own (C7f: 420 of 420 hand-ticks on 3 worlds);
  - step plus turnover = the unturned Meshes' rounding + Rule3's torque, exactly (C7g);
  - the rewind holds bit for bit (c3), and QR4 read forward only fails to rewind.
- OPEN: its cost. The hands beside a split top step one tick without that Mesh while the metric's sources keep it.

**5b. The weights' move.**
- lam = 1/(X p^2) belongs to the tick; X and p move with the regulator. So

      Q*(state; lam_t+1) - Q*(state; lam_t) = SUM_x (lam_t+1 - lam_t) d / 2.

- PROVED: c4 E2b, 84 of 84 ticks. The step keeps Q*_t within each tick (E2), and the chain breaks across ticks at 84
  of 84 (E2h).
- CHECKED on the head, per tick: up to 931,360 VSU on the hole, 4,542 on polarization and 1,066 on nature.
- The weight would stand still if X p^2 were one number everywhere. The vacuum's balance X p0^4 = Gamma^5 with
  p = p0^2 / Gamma gives X p^2 = Gamma^3. The load's step, the rates' rounding and F5's clamp (a load below 0 read at 0)
  break it: X p^2 / Gamma^3 runs from 0.034 to 1.058 on the hole and from 0.814 to 1.012 on nature.
- **QR3 (proposal): the Mesh's coupling read the same from both ends, the stretch inside.** The impulse X F becomes

      SUM_j G_j (m_j - a) - X D a,     G_j(x) = Q_j pinion (X_x p_x^2 + X_y p_y^2) = G_-j(x + e_j)

  This is the head's coupling wherever X p^2 is one number at the two ends. The charge's weight is then 1:
  Q = SUM_x d(x) / 2, kept across ticks whatever the rates do.
- PROVED: section 3 with lam = 1. CHECKED in the toy: E2, 84 of 84 ticks across; the unit weight with the head's
  coupling moves (E2-m1).
- CHECKED on the engine: the weights' term is 0 identically; every book closes bit for bit on hole and nature; the
  rewind holds bit for bit.
- OPEN: QR3 moves the hands where X p^2 differs between a Mesh's two ends. That is the metric's coupling, which the plan
  rewrites anyway.

**5c. Rule3's torque.**
- Per tick it is -SUM lam eps (tau + tau+) / 2, the notch step crossed with the fine teeth's offset. No quadratic charge
  of one state escapes it: B' (c1 A3) is exact but spans two states.
- PROVED.
- With QR1 to QR4 it is at most 13,710 VSU a tick on the hole, 2,414 on polarization and 362 on nature.
- A free hand (no Mesh, no spring) keeps its plain sweep bit for bit (c1 A5: 60,000 ticks). A lone sprung hand does
  not (A6: its sweep moved at 60,000 of 60,000 ticks).
- OPEN: book it as a remainder kept, as the wind's slack, or bound it.

**5d. The turn's rounding.**
- These are the two odd roundings of U a per Mesh. With QR1 to QR4 they are at most 613 VSU a tick on the hole, 562 on
  polarization and 39 on nature.
- They are exact only with an exact transport: U a as one Gaussian product over the Mesh's denominator L |g|^2 (the
  same from both ends; c1 B4 holds for any such U), and the six Meshes' numerators carried once with the stride.
- OPEN. The plan already writes "U_j a_j one complex product on integer coefficients".

**With QR1 to QR4** the step and the turnover move Q* by 5c and 5d alone. CHECKED: step plus turnover at most 13,696 VSU a
tick on the hole, 2,242 on polarization and 361 on nature (0.02 e).

## 6. The clicks: the charge book, which can fail and does

**The book.** Q* (form C) before against after every click. It is read at the next tick's weights, which the click does
not move (C9a). The change is attributed to the clocks the events name; nothing moves anywhere else (0 clicks).

**Takes (the chimes' credits).** They unwind the light, coupling 0.
- PROVED: the light carries no Q*.
- CHECKED: Q* kept at 4,201 of 4,201 (hole 1,299, nature 2,742, polarization 32, bang 128).

**Births (fittings) of a coupled hand.** Q* kept at 64 of 89: all 64 on the bang (a uniform board, no wall), 0 of 22 on
the hole, 0 of 3 on nature. Two exact causes:
1. **F1's senses keep the plain sweep, not the framed one.** With sigma_y = -sigma_x, a pair on a wall carries
   sigma e eps_x (lam_x W_x + lam_y W_y), about 2 e. This hit 10 of 22 on the hole.
   - The rewrite: the senses in the pawl frame, sigma_y = -f sigma_x.
   - PROVED: c4 E4, 20,000 draws; F1's senses fail on the walls (E4-m1).
2. **The fitted hand's plain sweep A B is one number at every clock, but Q* weighs it by lam W** (about X / Gamma, the
   stretch). The pair carries e (lam_x W_x - lam_y W_y) wherever the two ends' weights differ.
   - CHECKED: 22 of 22 on the hole and 3 of 3 on nature; up to 603,228 VSU on the hole even with the senses in the pawl
     frame, 781 on nature.
   - OPEN, and a contradiction with nature: the charge's quantum depends on the clock's stretch. Writing the quantum in
     the weight is no integer write, and booking the pair's remainder is the books' plug (E18).

**Unspringings.** Q* kept at 0 of 8 (hole 6, polarization 2). Two exact causes:
1. **The sense is read on the wrong board.** click.engage reads the plain sweeps of the tick's start, while the pair is
   unwound on the board after the turnover. On the hole, 3 of the 6 unwind two charges of one framed sign. The test must
   read the framed charge on the board it acts on.
2. **The evolved pair's weighted charges do not cancel**, because the step and the weights moved them. The unwind
   removes their sum. F5's gate in Q*'s form closes on almost every evolved pair, as draft A found for the plain sweep.

## 7. What it replaces

**R1 of the fakes** (click.py:95: a merger only as a conjugate pair, else jammed "sense").
- The plan's replacement reads "the sweep's conservation at the click, the light's sweep 0". It must read Q*, framed and
  weighted:
  - the merger's sense read in the pawl frame on the board it unwinds;
  - the lay's senses sigma_y = -f sigma_x.
- What stays OPEN is the gate eps_x lam_x d_x + eps_y lam_y d_y = 0. A fitted hand of fixed A B meets it only where the
  ends' weights agree; on the head's worlds that happens only on the uniform bang.

**The plan's "the charge's conservation derived from the one formula"** (TENSOR24.md section 19; section 16's draft A
row). It becomes:
- "The one formula keeps Q*_t = SUM eps Im(conj(P)(a + a-)) / (2 X p^2), tick by tick, to Rule3's torque and the turn's
  rounding, exactly where four conditions hold:
  - QR1, the current antisymmetric across every pawl wall, the triple read with the pawl;
  - QR2, the turnover turning the stored sweep with the pawl it flips;
  - QR3, the Mesh's coupling the same from both ends with the stretch inside, so that the charge is a run's and not a
    tick's;
  - QR4, the turned clock's Meshes at the teeth for the tick of its turnover, so that a split top creates no charge.
- The clicks keep it only where the lay's two ends share one frame and one weight. OPEN."
- In section 19's readings, the current is I_j(x) = eps S_j Im(conj(a) m_j). It is read at the tick's start notches,
  through the pawl and the turn, and its divergence closes the continuity. It is no two-time Wronskian across the Mesh.

**The law's clause "The sign is the rotation sense"** (ALGEBRA.md on scribe-5, line 721). The clause reads: "the charge
density at a Node-clock is the booking W_i = re_now x im_before - im_now x re_before, the Wronskian of the two pairs,
which Rule3's line conserves exactly with the conserved form's weights 1 / p_i^2 ..., its local change the Mesh current
G_ij = num (im_i re_j - re_i im_j) at the notches the step started from; ... its weighted total SUM_i W_i / p_i^2
exactly invariant in the rationals at rates fixed in time". Lines 799 and 803 say the same of the Wronskian and of
Omega(u, v).
- It holds for a free hand:
  - with no Mesh and no spring the plain sweep is kept bit for bit (c1 A5);
  - a lone sprung hand's is not (A6).
- On the coupled lattice:
  - the line keeps SUM eps (W/X) S / p^2: the law's 1/p^2, the stride's teeth over the stretch, and the pawl's sign the
    clause lacks;
  - it keeps it only where the Mesh current is antisymmetric (QR1);
  - the current is I_ij = eps_i S_ij Im(conj z_i U_ij K^f z_j), not G_ij;
  - in integers it keeps it to Rule3's torque, which is exact and booked;
  - "rates fixed in time" never holds on a running board, so across ticks the line keeps it only with QR3.
- The turnover's clause (line 1602, "the charge conjugated at every turned Node-clock") is what the head does, and QR2
  reverses it. Sofer words the sentence; the owner rules where it meets his time reversal (section 30, item 11).

## 8. OPEN, by name

1. **The clicks' quantum of charge against the weight.** The fitted hand's A B is one number at every clock, but Q*
   weighs it by lam W, about X / Gamma. The pair is neutral only where the ends' weights agree. This is a contradiction
   with nature on a curved board.
2. **Rule3's torque:** booked as a charge remainder, or bounded.
3. **The turn's rounding:** an exact transport, one Gaussian product per Mesh over the Mesh's denominator, the six Meshes
   carried once.
4. **QR2's consequences:**
   - the click's senses read in the pawl frame;
   - the retrace through a top, with the potentials' frame;
   - the twin hands, where draft E puts T = i sigma_y K.
5. **QR3 and QR4's dynamical cost:**
   - QR3 changes the metric's coupling where X p^2 differs across a Mesh;
   - QR4 keeps a Mesh in the regulator's sources that the hands drop for one tick.
6. **The stopped clocks:** p = 0, so their weight is 0 and their charge is parked; it re-enters at the restart with a
   jump. None stopped in these runs.
7. **The odd gears' frame, local or birth** (4a), and so which of QR1's two spellings is the potential's.

## 9. The probes and their outputs (this folder)

**c1_algebra.py, c1_algebra.txt.** Pure integers and exact rationals, with the engine's own acts.reverse:
- the continuity: A0 to A4, 60,000 each; the free hand A5, 60,000 ticks kept; the lone sprung hand A6, 60,000 of 60,000
  moved;
- the flux: B1 to B6, 40,000 Meshes (19,952 walls);
- Noether: C1 to C5, 24 tori;
- the turnover: D1 to D7, 60,000;
- 13 mutants, every one failing.

**c2_head.py with rewrites.py, c2_<variant>_<world><ticks>.txt.** The head's tick, bit for bit, with the flags --triple
(QR1), --turn (QR2), --r3 and --r4.
- The runs:
  - the head: hole 60, nature 60, polarization 30, bang 12;
  - QR1 and QR2 alone: hole;
  - QR1+QR2: hole, nature, polarization;
  - QR1+QR2+QR3: hole, nature;
  - QR1 to QR4: hole, nature, polarization.
- Every claim row holds at every count on all 14 runs.
- Its 3 mutants fail on every world with charge and walls: the torque dropped (M1), the pawl sign dropped (M2), and the
  turned clock's charge turned the other way (M3). On the bang (no wall, no charge) M2 cannot fail.

**c3_rewind.py, c3_rewind_hole30.txt and c3_rewind_nature30.txt.** The head, QR1, QR2, QR1+QR2, QR3, QR1+QR2+QR3, QR4 and QR1 to QR4
run 30 ticks forward and 30 back. Every integer of every train, the slack, the dial and the clicks return bit for bit,
over 841 (hole) and 751 (nature) turned clock-ticks. The mutants (QR2 with no involution, QR4 read forward only) fail to
rewind.

**c4_toy.py, c4_toy.txt.** The one formula in exact rationals on a 3^3 torus, over 14 ticks: E1 to E4, 5 mutants, every
one failing.

== common.py ==
"""Shared helpers for the charge probes: Gaussian integers as pairs, exact rationals, a tally that counts cases
and failures, and the engine's import (the repo's src, read only). Integers and Fractions only; no float enters
a check (floats are printed for sizes alone)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from fractions import Fraction

SRC = "/home/user/Universe24/src"
WORLDS = "/home/user/Universe24/tests/worlds"


def engine():
    if SRC not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, SRC)
    sys.set_int_max_str_digits(0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Gaussian pairs (re, im) = (three, twelve)
def gmul(p, q):
    return (p[0] * q[0] - p[1] * q[1], p[0] * q[1] + p[1] * q[0])


def gconj(p):
    return (p[0], -p[1])


def gadd(p, q):
    return (p[0] + q[0], p[1] + q[1])


def gsub(p, q):
    return (p[0] - q[0], p[1] - q[1])


def gscale(c, p):
    return (c * p[0], c * p[1])


def im_conj(a, b):
    """Im(conj(a) b) = a0 b1 - a1 b0 (meshes.cross's bilinear)."""
    return a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0]


def re_conj(a, b):
    """Re(conj(a) b) = a0 b0 + a1 b1 (hands.dot)."""
    return a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1]


def sweep(now, before):
    """hands.sweep: Im(conj(now) before) = three_now twelve_before - twelve_now three_before."""
    return now[0] * before[1] - now[1] * before[0]


def seen(z, f):
    """hands.seen: the neighbour read conjugated where the pawl f is -1."""
    return (z[0], f * z[1])


class Tally:
    """Counts cases and failures per named claim; a claim is reported as 'runs N failures F'."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.rows: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
        self.order: list[str] = []

    def __call__(self, key, ok, by=1):
        if key not in self.rows:
            self.rows[key] = [0, 0]
            self.order.append(key)
        self.rows[key][0] += by
        self.rows[key][1] += 0 if ok else by

    def fails(self, key):
        return self.rows.get(key, [0, 0])[1]

    def runs(self, key):
        return self.rows.get(key, [0, 0])[0]

    def report(self, out=print, width=96):
        for key in self.order:
            runs, fails = self.rows[key]
            out(f"  {key:{width}s} runs {runs:8d} failures {fails}")


def F(x):
    return Fraction(x)

== rewrites.py ==
"""The rewrites as patches on the head's engine in memory (no file of the repo is touched), one switch each:
QR1  the Mesh's triple turn summed through the pawl: the odd gear side (A_x + f A_y) and the flow gears' skew
    3 K side (V_x + f V_y), as the offset's count K_x - f K_y already is (tick.meshes_of, tick.skewed);
QR2  the turnover exchanges a hand's two slots with no K (hands.signs [1, 1] at clock.reverse), so its stored sweep
    turns with the pawl the turnover flips;
QR3  every coupled hand's impulse X F read with the Mesh's coupling the same from both ends, the stretch inside:
    XF' = SUM_j G_j (m_j - a) - X D a, G_j(x) = Q_j pinion (X_x p_x^2 + X_y p_y^2) = G_-j(x + e_j) (the head's
    X(x) 2 p(x)^2 Q_j pinion where X p^2 is one number at both ends; the head's pull kept for the forms and release).
QR4  the turned clock's Meshes at the teeth for the tick of its turnover: the trigger (X0 <= X1 >= X2 with one strict,
    or below the floor) read on the regulator's act before the driven trains' carry (X2 is the regulator's own notch,
    which no later act of the tick moves; a stopped clock reads its restored X1), and at every clock beside a turned
    one the hands' impulse read with that Mesh's term taken out (the regulator's sources keep every Mesh); backward
    the turned clocks are the rewind note's, so the same impulse is read;
apply(r1, r2, r3, r4) sets them; apply() restores the head."""
from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
import textwrap

from event_universe import hands, readings
from event_universe import tick as tickmod
from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES, SPOKE_SIDES, SPOKES, across
from event_universe.meshes import Mesh, coupling_of
from event_universe.regulation import rest_rate_of

ORIG = dict(meshes_of=tickmod.meshes_of, skewed=tickmod.skewed, signs=hands.signs,
            pulls=tickmod.Tick.pulls, impulse=tickmod.Tick.impulse, carry=tickmod.Tick.carry)


def meshes_through_pawl(factors, pawl_signs, offset_pairs, odd):
    found = []
    for spoke, (axis, side) in enumerate(SPOKE_SIDES):
        f = pawl_signs[spoke]
        found.append(Mesh(factors[spoke], f, *offset_pairs[spoke], side * (odd[axis] + f * across(odd[axis], spoke))))
    return found


def skewed_through_pawl(meshes, rest_rate, flow_gears, full_rate):
    w2 = full_rate * full_rate
    found = []
    for spoke, (axis, side) in enumerate(SPOKE_SIDES):
        at = meshes[spoke]
        m = AXES * rest_rate * side * (flow_gears[axis] + at.pawl * across(flow_gears[axis], spoke))
        found.append(Mesh(at.factor, at.pawl, at.cosine, at.sine, at.odd, (w2, m)))
    return found


def signs_turned(size):
    return [1] * size


def pulls_r3(self):
    ORIG["pulls"](self)
    clk = self.clock
    self.xf = {}
    xp2 = self.stretch_now * self.mesh_rate * self.mesh_rate
    for r, cpl in self.hand_couplings:
        if cpl != 1:
            continue
        k = self.where[id(r)]
        gearing = readings.gearing_of(clk, r, self.load, self.factors)
        turn = tickmod.skewed(self.meshes_at[1], coupling_of(r) * rest_rate_of(clk, r.ratio), self.flow_gears, clk.full_rate)
        a = self.starts[k]
        ms = [clk.offset_pull([across(x, j) for x in a], j, turn) for j in range(SPOKES)]
        G = [self.factors[j] * r.ratio[0] * (xp2 + across(xp2, j)) for j in range(SPOKES)]
        self.xf[k] = [sum(G[j] * (ms[j][g] - a[g]) for j in range(SPOKES)) - self.stretch_now * gearing.spring * a[g]
                      for g in range(2)]


def impulse_r3(r4=False):
    src = textwrap.dedent(inspect.getsource(ORIG["impulse"]))
    if r4:
        side = ("        p_side = [\n            w_side * (gear.now - gear.before) + gear.fine - mid(w_side) for gear in self.trains[k]\n"
                "        ]\n")
        assert side in src
        src = src.replace(side, side + "        if k in getattr(self, 'mask_bwd', {}):\n"
                          "            p_side = [p + t for p, t in zip(p_side, self.mask_bwd[k], strict=True)]\n")
    block = ("        p_other = [\n            p + self.going * self.stretch_now * f for p, f in zip(p_side, self.pull[k], strict=True)\n"
             "        ]\n")
    assert block in src
    new = ("        if k in getattr(self, 'xf', {}):\n"
           "            p_other = [p + self.going * f for p, f in zip(p_side, self.xf[k], strict=True)]\n"
           "        else:\n" + block.replace("        p_other", "            p_other", 1)
           .replace("\n            p + ", "\n                p + ").replace("\n        ]", "\n            ]"))
    ns = dict(vars(tickmod))
    exec(compile(src.replace(block, new), "<impulse_r3>", "exec"), ns)
    return ns["impulse"]


IMPULSE_R3 = impulse_r3()
IMPULSE_R34 = impulse_r3(r4=True)
R3_ON = [False]


def taken_terms(self, top):
    """The Mesh terms QR4 takes out of a coupled hand's impulse at every clock beside a turned one, read from the
    tick's start alone (the starts, the meshes, the grips or QR3's G), the same integers forward and backward."""
    import numpy as np
    clk = self.clock
    found = {}
    if not top.any():
        return found
    xp2 = self.stretch_now * self.mesh_rate * self.mesh_rate
    for r, cpl in self.hand_couplings:
        if cpl != 1:
            continue
        k = self.where[id(r)]
        gearing = readings.gearing_of(clk, r, self.load, self.factors)
        turn = tickmod.skewed(self.meshes_at[1], coupling_of(r) * rest_rate_of(clk, r.ratio), self.flow_gears, clk.full_rate)
        a = self.starts[k]
        taken = [0 * a[0], 0 * a[1]]
        for j in range(SPOKES):
            beside = (~top) & across(top, j)
            if not beside.any():
                continue
            m = clk.offset_pull([across(x, j) for x in a], j, turn)
            if getattr(self, "xf", None) is not None and k in self.xf:
                c = self.factors[j] * r.ratio[0] * (xp2 + across(xp2, j))
            else:
                c = self.stretch_now * gearing.grips[j]
            for g in range(2):
                taken[g] = taken[g] + np.where(beside, c * (m[g] - a[g]), 0)
        found[k] = taken
    return found


def pulls_r4(self):
    """QR4 backward: the turned clocks are the note's, known at the tick's making, so the terms are read here and the
    impulse adds them back to the stored stride before any source is read (the sources the forward read)."""
    (pulls_r3 if R3_ON[0] else ORIG["pulls"])(self)
    import numpy as np
    self.mask_bwd = {}
    if self.going == -1:
        top = np.asarray(self.top, dtype=bool) * np.ones(self.clock.size, dtype=bool)
        self.mask_bwd = taken_terms(self, top)


def mask_turned(self, w_div):
    """QR4's act inside the carry: the turned clocks known from the regulator's act (forward) or the note (backward),
    and the impelled strides of every coupled hand beside them read with those Meshes' terms taken out."""
    import numpy as np
    from event_universe.trains import REGULATOR
    clk = self.clock
    if self.going == 1:
        k = self.where[id(self.regulator)]
        x0, x1 = self.held[k][0].before, self.held[k][0].now
        x2 = np.where(self.stopped, x1, self.out[k][0].now)
        xf = readings.bottom(clk)
        top = (x0 <= x1) & (x2 <= x1) & ((x0 < x1) | (x2 < x1)) | (x0 < xf) | (x2 < xf)
    else:
        top = self.top
    top = np.asarray(top, dtype=bool) * np.ones(clk.size, dtype=bool)
    self.predicted_top = top
    self.masked = {}
    if self.going != 1:
        return
    self.masked = taken_terms(self, top)
    for k, taken in self.masked.items():
        self.impelled[k] = [p - t for p, t in zip(self.impelled[k], taken, strict=True)]


def carry_r4():
    src = textwrap.dedent(inspect.getsource(ORIG["carry"]))
    line = "    w_div = self.end_teeth(self.out[k][0])\n"
    assert line in src
    ns = dict(vars(tickmod))
    ns["mask_turned"] = mask_turned
    exec(compile(src.replace(line, line + "    mask_turned(self, w_div)\n"), "<carry_r4>", "exec"), ns)
    method = ns["carry"]
    ns["carry"] = vars(tickmod)["carry"]  # the method's own name rebound to Rule3's carry, which its body calls
    return method


CARRY_R4 = carry_r4()


def apply(r1=False, r2=False, r3=False, r4=False):
    tickmod.meshes_of = meshes_through_pawl if r1 else ORIG["meshes_of"]
    tickmod.skewed = skewed_through_pawl if r1 else ORIG["skewed"]
    hands.signs = signs_turned if r2 else ORIG["signs"]
    R3_ON[0] = r3
    tickmod.Tick.pulls = pulls_r4 if r4 else pulls_r3 if r3 else ORIG["pulls"]
    tickmod.Tick.impulse = IMPULSE_R34 if r4 else IMPULSE_R3 if r3 else ORIG["impulse"]
    tickmod.Tick.carry = CARRY_R4 if r4 else ORIG["carry"]

== c1_algebra.py ==
"""c1: the charge's algebra in integers and exact rationals, every claim counted, every mutant made to fail.

A. The step's continuity per Node-clock (the one formula's letter: P = W (a - a-) + (r - mid W), P+ = P + X F,
   a+ = a + (P+ + mid W+) div W+, r+ = (P+ + mid W+) mod W+, Rule3's one carry), on random integers of both teeth
   parities, of both signs and up to 200 bits:
     (A)  W+ S(a+, a) - W S(a, a-) = -X Im(conj a F) + Im(conj(rho) a) - Im(conj(rho+) a)       [the sweep's form]
     (B)  Im(conj a+ P+) - Im(conj a P) = X Im(conj a F) + Im(conj(v) rho+)                      [the stride's form]
     (B') Im(conj a P+) - Im(conj a P) = X Im(conj a F)                                           [no remainder]
   with S(n, b) = Im(conj n b) the sweep, rho = r - mid W, v = a+ - a.
B. The Mesh's flux, exact rationals: I_xy = eps_x S_xy Im(conj(a_x) U_xy a_y), U_xy = o_xy t_xy K^f (the offset's
   pair at the count n_xy = K_x - f K_y, the odd gear's and the skew's tangent triple, the pawl's reflection), the
   head's triple (odd_xy = side (A_x + A_y), m_xy = 3 K side (V_x + V_y)) against the rewrite through the pawl
   (odd_xy = side (A_x + f A_y), m_xy = 3 K side (V_x + f V_y)); I_xy + I_yx = 0, and on a wall the head's exact
   defect 2 eps_x S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)).
C. Noether on a random torus: the step's force F commutes with the pawl-framed phase z(x) -> i^eps(x) z(x) on the
   head and on the rewrite alike, but the pairing SUM_x eps lam X Im(conj a F), lam = 1 / (X p^2), is 0 only on
   the rewrite (the force self-adjoint in the framed product), and the operator lam X F symmetric only there.
D. The turnover on one hand (the engine's own acts.reverse and mirror): the head (hands.signs [1, -1]) keeps the
   stored sweep while the pawl flips, so eps S reverses; the rewrite (signs [1, 1]) turns the sweep with the pawl,
   eps S kept; both involutions; the stride P -> -conj P on the head, -P on the rewrite.
Each section carries mutants that must fail.  Run: venv314/bin/python -I c1_algebra.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import random
import sys
from fractions import Fraction

HERE = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/charge"
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from common import Tally, engine, gconj, gmul, im_conj, re_conj, sweep  # noqa: E402

engine()
from event_universe.core import acts  # noqa: E402

rng = random.Random(20261028)
T = Tally()
M = Tally()  # the mutants: each row must show failures


def mid(w):
    return w // 2


def carry(total, teeth, fine):  # acts.carry at going 1: (total + r) div w and mod w
    u = total + fine
    return u // teeth, u % teeth


def rnd(bits):
    return rng.randrange(-2 ** bits, 2 ** bits)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- A. continuity
def draw_state():
    bits = rng.choice((8, 20, 64, 200))
    W = rng.randrange(1, 2 ** rng.choice((3, 12, 40, 90)))
    Wp = rng.choice((W, W + rng.randrange(0, 50), rng.randrange(1, 2 ** rng.choice((3, 12, 40, 90)))))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        W, Wp = W + (W & 1 == 0), Wp + (Wp & 1)  # both parities of the teeth
    X = rng.randrange(1, 2 ** rng.choice((4, 20, 50)))
    a = (rnd(bits), rnd(bits))
    am = (a[0] + rnd(bits // 2 + 1), a[1] + rnd(bits // 2 + 1))
    r = (rng.randrange(W), rng.randrange(W))
    Fp = (rnd(bits + 10), rnd(bits + 10))
    return W, Wp, X, a, am, r, Fp


def step(W, Wp, X, a, am, r, Fp, sign=1):
    P = tuple(W * (x - y) + (q - mid(W)) for x, y, q in zip(a, am, r))
    Pp = tuple(p + sign * X * f for p, f in zip(P, Fp))
    vr = [carry(p, Wp, mid(Wp)) for p in Pp]
    v = tuple(q for q, _ in vr)
    rp = tuple(q for _, q in vr)
    ap = tuple(x + q for x, q in zip(a, v))
    rho = tuple(q - mid(W) for q in r)
    rhop = tuple(q - mid(Wp) for q in rp)
    return P, Pp, v, rp, ap, rho, rhop


for _ in range(60000):
    W, Wp, X, a, am, r, Fp = draw_state()
    P, Pp, v, rp, ap, rho, rhop = step(W, Wp, X, a, am, r, Fp)
    # the carry's own letter: P+ = W+ v + rho+ exactly
    T("A0 the stride after the carry: P+ = W+ (a+ - a) + (r+ - mid W+)", Pp == tuple(Wp * q + h for q, h in zip(v, rhop)))
    lhs_a = Wp * sweep(ap, a) - W * sweep(a, am)
    rhs_a = -X * im_conj(a, Fp) + im_conj(rho, a) - im_conj(rhop, a)
    T("A1 (A) W+ S(a+,a) - W S(a,a-) = -X Im(conj a F) + Im(conj rho a) - Im(conj rho+ a)", lhs_a == rhs_a)
    lhs_b = im_conj(ap, Pp) - im_conj(a, P)
    T("A2 (B) Im(conj a+ P+) - Im(conj a P) = X Im(conj a F) + Im(conj v rho+)", lhs_b == X * im_conj(a, Fp) + im_conj(v, rhop))
    T("A3 (B') Im(conj a P+) - Im(conj a P) = X Im(conj a F), no remainder term", im_conj(a, Pp) - im_conj(a, P) == X * im_conj(a, Fp))
    # the two densities differ by the fine teeth's term: -Im(conj a P) = W S + Im(conj rho a)
    T("A4 -Im(conj a P) = W S(a, a-) + Im(conj rho a)", -im_conj(a, P) == W * sweep(a, am) + im_conj(rho, a))
    # mutants
    M("A1-m1 (A) without the remainders' terms", lhs_a == -X * im_conj(a, Fp))
    M("A1-m2 (A) with rho+ centred on mid W (the start's teeth)", lhs_a == -X * im_conj(a, Fp) + im_conj(rho, a)
      - im_conj(tuple(q - mid(W) for q in rp), a))
    M("A2-m3 (B) without the carry's torque Im(conj v rho+)", lhs_b == X * im_conj(a, Fp))
    P2, Pp2, v2, rp2, ap2, rho2, rhop2 = step(W, Wp, X, a, am, r, Fp, sign=-1)
    M("A1-m4 (A) with the impulse's sign flipped (P+ = P - X F)", Wp * sweep(ap2, a) - W * sweep(a, am) == rhs_a)

# the free hand: F = 0 and fixed teeth keep the sweep exactly (the law's clause holds there) ...
free_bad = free_n = 0
spring_moved = spring_n = 0
for _ in range(3000):
    W = rng.randrange(2, 10 ** 6)
    X = rng.randrange(1, 10 ** 3)
    a = (rnd(30), rnd(30))
    am = (a[0] + rnd(10), a[1] + rnd(10))
    r = (rng.randrange(W), rng.randrange(W))
    S0 = sweep(a, am)
    st = (a, am, r)
    for _t in range(20):
        a_, am_, r_ = st
        P, Pp, v, rp, ap, rho, rhop = step(W, W, X, a_, am_, r_, (0, 0))
        st = (ap, a_, rp)
        free_n += 1
        free_bad += sweep(ap, a_) != S0
    # ... and a single sprung hand (F = -D a, no meshes) does not, at the integers, by the remainders alone
    D = rng.randrange(1, 50)
    st = (a, am, r)
    S0 = sweep(a, am)
    for _t in range(20):
        a_, am_, r_ = st
        P, Pp, v, rp, ap, rho, rhop = step(W, W, X, a_, am_, r_, (-D * a_[0], -D * a_[1]))
        st = (ap, a_, rp)
        spring_n += 1
        spring_moved += sweep(ap, a_) != S0
T("A5 the free hand (F = 0, fixed teeth): the plain sweep kept bit for bit, tick by tick", free_bad == 0, free_n)
print(f"[A6] a lone sprung hand (F = -D a, no Mesh): the plain sweep moved at {spring_moved} of {spring_n} ticks"
      " (the remainders' term; the law's 'conserved' holds in the rationals and to the carried remainders only)")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- B. the flux
def tangent(W2, n):  # (W2^2 - n^2, 2 W2 n) over W2^2 + n^2: an exact unit, even/odd in n
    return (W2 * W2 - n * n, 2 * W2 * n), W2 * W2 + n * n


def unit_of(pair, norm):
    return (Fraction(pair[0], norm), Fraction(pair[1], norm))


def triple(wl, w2, odd, m):  # meshes.through_mesh's g = (wl w2 - odd m, wl m + odd w2); its turn g^2 / |g|^2
    g3, g12 = wl * w2 - odd * m, wl * m + odd * w2
    sq = (g3 * g3 - g12 * g12, 2 * g3 * g12)
    return unit_of(sq, g3 * g3 + g12 * g12), (g3, g12)


def flux(eps_x, S, ax, U, ay, f):
    """eps_x S Im(conj(a_x) U K^f a_y) as an exact rational."""
    y = ay if f == 1 else gconj(ay)
    return eps_x * S * im_conj(ax, gmul(U, y))


wl, w2 = 4 * 1000, 1000 * 1000
walls_head_bad = 0
for _ in range(40000):
    f = rng.choice((1, -1))
    eps_x = rng.choice((1, -1))
    eps_y = f * eps_x
    side = rng.choice((1, -1))
    S = rng.randrange(1, 10 ** 6)
    ax, ay = (rnd(40), rnd(40)), (rnd(40), rnd(40))
    Kx, Ky = rnd(12), rnd(12)
    Ax, Ay, Vx, Vy = rnd(10), rnd(10), rnd(10), rnd(10)
    Kr = rng.randrange(0, 5000)
    Mo = rng.randrange(1, 4000)
    # the offset's count read through the pawl, from both ends (meshes.offsets: n_ij = K_i - f_ij K_j)
    o_xy = unit_of(*tangent(Mo, Kx - f * Ky))
    o_yx = unit_of(*tangent(Mo, Ky - f * Kx))
    # the head's triple: the odd and flow gears summed with the spoke's side, no pawl (meshes.meshes_of, skewed)
    t_xy, _ = triple(wl, w2, side * (Ax + Ay), 3 * Kr * side * (Vx + Vy))
    t_yx, _ = triple(wl, w2, -side * (Ay + Ax), 3 * Kr * -side * (Vy + Vx))
    # the rewrite: summed through the pawl, as the count is
    r_xy, _ = triple(wl, w2, side * (Ax + f * Ay), 3 * Kr * side * (Vx + f * Vy))
    r_yx, _ = triple(wl, w2, -side * (Ay + f * Ax), 3 * Kr * -side * (Vy + f * Vx))
    head = flux(eps_x, S, ax, gmul(o_xy, t_xy), ay, f) + flux(eps_y, S, ay, gmul(o_yx, t_yx), ax, f)
    new = flux(eps_x, S, ax, gmul(o_xy, r_xy), ay, f) + flux(eps_y, S, ay, gmul(o_yx, r_yx), ax, f)
    if f == 1:
        T("B1 head, one frame (f = +1): I_xy + I_yx = 0", head == 0)
    else:
        walls_head_bad += head != 0
        # Re(o conj(a_x a_y)): o times conj of the product a_x a_y, its real part
        prod = gmul(ax, ay)
        re_part = re_conj((1, 0), gmul(o_xy, gconj(prod)))
        T("B2 head, a pawl wall (f = -1): I_xy + I_yx = 2 eps_x S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)) exactly", head == 2 * eps_x * S * t_xy[1] * re_part)
        T("B3 head, a pawl wall: the offset pair alone the same from both ends (o_yx = o_xy)", o_yx == o_xy)
    T("B4 the rewrite through the pawl, every Mesh: I_xy + I_yx = 0", new == 0)
    T("B5 the offset pair: Re o_yx = Re o_xy and Im o_yx = -f Im o_xy", o_yx[0] == o_xy[0] and o_yx[1] == -f * o_xy[1])
    T("B6 the rewritten triple: Re t_yx = Re t_xy and Im t_yx = -f Im t_xy", r_yx[0] == r_xy[0] and r_yx[1] == -f * r_xy[1])
    # mutants
    if f == -1:
        o_bad = unit_of(*tangent(Mo, Ky - Kx))  # the count with no pawl
        o_bad_x = unit_of(*tangent(Mo, Kx - Ky))
        M("B-m1 the count with no pawl across a wall (n = K_x - K_y)",
          flux(eps_x, S, ax, gmul(o_bad_x, r_xy), ay, f) + flux(eps_y, S, ay, gmul(o_bad, r_yx), ax, f) == 0)
        h_xy, _ = triple(wl, w2, side * (Ax + f * Ay), 3 * Kr * side * (Vx + Vy))
        h_yx, _ = triple(wl, w2, -side * (Ay + f * Ax), 3 * Kr * -side * (Vy + Vx))
        M("B-m2 the odd gear through the pawl, the skew not",
          flux(eps_x, S, ax, gmul(o_xy, h_xy), ay, f) + flux(eps_y, S, ay, gmul(o_yx, h_yx), ax, f) == 0)
        M("B-m3 the rewrite read with no pawl sign (eps dropped) across a wall",
          flux(1, S, ax, gmul(o_xy, r_xy), ay, f) + flux(1, S, ay, gmul(o_yx, r_yx), ax, f) == 0)
    Sx, Sy = S * rng.randrange(1, 5) ** 2, S * rng.randrange(1, 5) ** 2
    if Sx != Sy:
        M("B-m4 the grips unweighted by the rates (S_x != S_y)",
          flux(eps_x, Sx, ax, gmul(o_xy, r_xy), ay, f) + flux(eps_y, Sy, ay, gmul(o_yx, r_yx), ax, f) == 0)
print(f"[B7] the head's triple across a pawl wall: I_xy + I_yx != 0 at {walls_head_bad} of "
      f"{T.runs('B2 head, a pawl wall (f = -1): I_xy + I_yx = 2 eps_x S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)) exactly')} walls")

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- C. Noether
L = 3
SITES = list(itertools.product(range(L), repeat=3))
SPOKES = [(a, s) for a in range(3) for s in (1, -1)]


def nb(x, a, s):
    y = list(x)
    y[a] = (y[a] + s) % L
    return tuple(y)


def build(rule, field):
    """The step's exact force (no rounding) at every site, with the head's or the rewritten triple."""
    eps, Kc, A, V, S, p, X, Dsp, Kr, Mo = (field[k] for k in ("eps", "K", "A", "V", "S", "p", "X", "D", "Kr", "Mo"))

    def U(x, j):
        a, s = SPOKES[j]
        y = nb(x, a, s)
        f = eps[x] * eps[y]
        o = unit_of(*tangent(Mo, Kc[x] - f * Kc[y]))
        if rule == "head":
            odd, m = s * (A[x][a] + A[y][a]), 3 * Kr * s * (V[x][a] + V[y][a])
        else:
            odd, m = s * (A[x][a] + f * A[y][a]), 3 * Kr * s * (V[x][a] + f * V[y][a])
        t, _ = triple(wl, w2, odd, m)
        return y, f, gmul(o, t)

    links = {(x, j): U(x, j) for x in SITES for j in range(6)}

    def force(z):
        out = {}
        for x in SITES:
            acc = (Fraction(0), Fraction(0))
            for j, (a, s) in enumerate(SPOKES):
                y, f, u = links[(x, j)]
                zy = z[y] if f == 1 else gconj(z[y])
                g = p[x] * p[x] * S[(x, j)]
                m = gmul(u, zy)
                acc = (acc[0] + g * (m[0] - z[x][0]), acc[1] + g * (m[1] - z[x][1]))
            acc = (acc[0] - Dsp[x] * z[x][0], acc[1] - Dsp[x] * z[x][1])
            out[x] = acc
        return out

    return force


def random_field():
    bits = {x: rng.randint(0, 1) for x in SITES}
    eps = {x: 1 - 2 * bits[x] for x in SITES}
    S = {}
    for x in SITES:
        for j, (a, s) in enumerate(SPOKES):
            if s == 1:
                S[(x, j)] = rng.randrange(1, 9)
                S[(nb(x, a, 1), j + 1)] = S[(x, j)]
    return dict(eps=eps, K={x: rnd(6) for x in SITES}, A={x: [rnd(6) for _ in range(3)] for x in SITES},
                V={x: [rnd(6) for _ in range(3)] for x in SITES}, S=S,
                p={x: rng.randrange(300, 1001) for x in SITES}, X={x: rng.randrange(500, 1501) for x in SITES},
                D={x: rng.randrange(0, 40) for x in SITES}, Kr=rng.randrange(1, 3000), Mo=rng.randrange(50, 3000))


def i_eps(z, eps):  # multiplication by i^eps(x) at each site: the pawl-framed quarter phase
    return {x: ((-v[1], v[0]) if eps[x] == 1 else (v[1], -v[0])) for x, v in z.items()}


for trial in range(24):
    fld = random_field()
    z = {x: (Fraction(rnd(30)), Fraction(rnd(30))) for x in SITES}
    w = {x: (Fraction(rnd(30)), Fraction(rnd(30))) for x in SITES}
    lam = {x: Fraction(1, fld["X"][x] * fld["p"][x] ** 2) for x in SITES}
    for rule in ("head", "rewrite"):
        Fz = build(rule, fld)
        fz = Fz(z)
        fi = Fz(i_eps(z, fld["eps"]))
        T(f"C1 {rule}: the force commutes with the framed phase, F(i^eps z) = i^eps F(z) at every site",
          fi == i_eps(fz, fld["eps"]))
        pairing = sum(fld["eps"][x] * lam[x] * fld["X"][x] * im_conj(z[x], fz[x]) for x in SITES)
        fw = Fz(w)
        sym = sum(lam[x] * fld["X"][x] * re_conj(w[x], fz[x]) for x in SITES) - sum(
            lam[x] * fld["X"][x] * re_conj(z[x], fw[x]) for x in SITES)
        if rule == "rewrite":
            T("C2 rewrite: Noether's pairing SUM eps lam X Im(conj z F(z)) = 0, lam = 1/(X p^2)", pairing == 0)
            T("C3 rewrite: lam X F symmetric, <w, lam X F z> = <z, lam X F w>", sym == 0)
            M("C-m1 the pairing with lam = 1/p^2 on the impulse X F (the stretch dropped from lam)",
              sum(fld["eps"][x] * Fraction(1, fld["p"][x] ** 2) * fld["X"][x] * im_conj(z[x], fz[x]) for x in SITES) == 0)
            plain_i = {x: (-v[1], v[0]) for x, v in z.items()}
            M("C-m4 the plain phase z -> i z with no pawl frame: F(i z) = i F(z)",
              Fz(plain_i) == {x: (-v[1], v[0]) for x, v in fz.items()})
            M("C-m2 the pairing with lam = 1/X (the mesh rate dropped)",
              sum(fld["eps"][x] * im_conj(z[x], fz[x]) for x in SITES) == 0)
            M("C-m3 the pairing with no pawl sign",
              sum(lam[x] * fld["X"][x] * im_conj(z[x], fz[x]) for x in SITES) == 0)
        else:
            T("C4 head: Noether's pairing is not 0 where walls and odd gears stand (counted as failures)", pairing == 0)
            T("C5 head: lam X F not symmetric (counted as failures)", sym == 0)

# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- D. the turnover
class G:
    def __init__(self, now, before, fine, teeth):
        self.now, self.before, self.fine, self.teeth = now, before, fine, teeth


edge = 0
for _ in range(60000):
    w_ = rng.randrange(2, 2 ** rng.choice((4, 16, 60)))
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        w_ |= 1
    n3, n12, b3, b12 = (rnd(40) for _ in range(4))
    f3, f12 = rng.randrange(w_), rng.randrange(w_)
    if rng.random() < 0.1:
        f3 = 0
    held = [G(n3, b3, f3, w_), G(n12, b12, f12, w_)]
    bit = rng.randint(0, 1)
    eps, eps2 = 1 - 2 * bit, 1 - 2 * (1 - bit)  # the ratchet's fine tooth flipped at two teeth
    S0 = n3 * b12 - n12 * b3
    P0 = (w_ * (n3 - b3) + f3 - w_ // 2, w_ * (n12 - b12) + f12 - w_ // 2)
    for rule, sg in (("head", [1, -1]), ("rewrite", [1, 1])):
        nows, befores, fines = acts.reverse(held, sg)
        S1 = nows[0] * befores[1] - nows[1] * befores[0]
        P1 = (w_ * (nows[0] - befores[0]) + fines[0] - w_ // 2, w_ * (nows[1] - befores[1]) + fines[1] - w_ // 2)
        back = acts.reverse([G(nows[0], befores[0], fines[0], w_), G(nows[1], befores[1], fines[1], w_)], sg)
        T(f"D1 {rule}: the turnover an involution on the hand (notches and fine teeth)",
          back == ([n3, n12], [b3, b12], [f3, f12]))
        odd_edge = (w_ % 2 == 0) and (f3 == 0 or (rule == "rewrite" and f12 == 0))
        if rule == "head":
            T("D2 head: the stored sweep kept, S' = S", S1 == S0)
            T("D3 head: the framed sweep reversed, eps' S' = -eps S (Q* reversed at the turned clock)", eps2 * S1 == -eps * S0)
            if not odd_edge:
                T("D4 head: the stride P' = -conj P (off the even-teeth edge, fine tooth 0)", P1 == (-P0[0], P0[1]))
        else:
            T("D5 rewrite: the sweep turned with the pawl, S' = -S", S1 == -S0)
            T("D6 rewrite: the framed sweep kept, eps' S' = eps S", eps2 * S1 == eps * S0)
            if not odd_edge:
                T("D7 rewrite: the stride P' = -P (off the even-teeth edge)", P1 == (-P0[0], -P0[1]))
            else:
                edge += 1
            M("D-m1 the rewrite read with the pawl not flipped (eps' = eps)", eps * S1 == eps * S0)
print(f"[D8] the even-teeth edge (a fine tooth 0 at even teeth, where the one mirror misses -P by W): {edge} draws set aside in D7")

print("c1, the charge's algebra (integers and exact rationals)")
T.report()
print("mutants (each must show failures):")
M.report()
bad = [k for k in M.order if M.fails(k) == 0]
print("every mutant failed" if not bad else f"MUTANTS THAT DID NOT FAIL: {bad}")

== c2_head.py ==
"""c2: Q* through every act of the head's tick, bit for bit, on worlds with pawl walls (a read-only harness).

Q*_t = SUM_x lam_t(x) q(x), lam = 1/(X p^2) (X the stretch now, p the mesh rate at the clock's own load: the two
numbers the step's impulse X F and its grips 2 p^2 Q pinion read), q one of three densities of a hand at a clock,
eps = 1 - 2 b the pawl's sign (b the ratchet's fine tooth), W the hand gear's teeth, S = Im(conj(now) before) the
sweep, rho = r - mid W the fine teeth's offset, P = W (now - before) + rho the stride:
   A  q = eps W S                                   (the notches alone)
   B  q = -eps Im(conj(now) P)       = eps (W S + Im(conj rho now))           (the verifier's)
   C  q = -eps Im(conj(now + before) P) / 2 = eps (W S + Im(conj rho (now + before)) / 2)   (the stride's midpoint)
Per tick and coupled hand (coupling 1), integers and Fractions only:
  C1  the step's continuity of each form at every live clock, bit for bit (v = now - before, v+ = now+ - now):
      dA = eps[-X Im(conj a F) + Im(conj rho a) - Im(conj rho+ a)],  dB = eps[-X Im(conj a F) - Im(conj v+ rho+)],
      dC = eps[-X Im(conj a F) - (Im(conj v+ rho+) + Im(conj v rho)) / 2]
  C3  Im(conj a F) = p^2 SUM_j S_j Im(conj a m_j), S_j = 2 Q_j pinion, m_j the engine's own turned neighbour
  C4  the Mesh's integer flux I_j(x) = eps(x) S_j(x) Im(conj a(x) m_j(x)), its defect T = I_j(x) + I_-j(x + e_j)
  C5  T = T_pawl + T_round: T_pawl the exact (unrounded) transport's, U = (c + i s)/L g^2/|g|^2 K^f, equal to
      2 eps_x S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)) on a wall (head) and 0 elsewhere; T_round the two roundings of the turn
  C6  the step's book per form: dQ*_step = -SUM_Meshes T + SUM_x lam (the form's carry term), exactly
  C7  the turnover at every turned clock: q' against q of the tick's start, per form (head: A and C reversed; with
      --turn: A and C kept); its book dQ*_turn = SUM_turned lam (q' - q_start) + the discarded step, and the
      discarded step's flux = the flux out of the turned region + its inner Meshes' defects
  C8  the weights' move: dQ*_bg = SUM_x (lam_t+1 - lam_t) q' (turned clocks apart)
  C9  the click: dQ*_click with the next tick's weights (unchanged by the click: checked), per click and event
  C10 the chain: Q*(start t+1) = Q*(start t) + step + turn + bg + click, exactly, per form
Units: VSU, Q* times Gamma^2 / T: a fitted hand at the vacuum carries A B = e = 18,000 VSU.
Flags: --triple (QR1, the odd gear and the skew summed through the pawl), --turn (QR2, the turnover exchanges a
hand's slots with no K, its stored sweep turned with the pawl), --r3 (QR3, the Mesh's coupling the same from both ends
with the stretch inside, the charge's weight 1); the patches in rewrites.py.
Run: venv314/bin/python -I c2_head.py world ticks [--triple] [--turn] [--r3]
"""
