def iv(a, x):
    return int(a[x]) if np.ndim(a) else int(a)


def nb(x, j):
    a, s = SPOKE_SIDES[j]
    y = list(x)
    y[a] = (y[a] + s) % size[a]
    return tuple(y)


def weights(clk):
    """lam = 1/(X p^2) and eps at every clock from the state as the next tick's start reads it (lam 0 where p = 0)."""
    tg = clk.train(REGULATOR).gears[0]
    load = readings.load(clk)
    p = np.asarray(readings.rates_of(clk, load)[1], dtype=object) * np.ones(size, dtype=object)
    X = np.asarray(tg.now, dtype=object)
    eps = 1 - 2 * np.asarray(ratchet_of(clk.trains_).gears[0].fine, dtype=object)
    if QR3:
        lam = {x: (Fraction(1) if int(p[x]) else Fraction(0)) for x in SITES}
    else:
        lam = {x: (Fraction(1, int(X[x]) * int(p[x]) ** 2) if int(p[x]) else Fraction(0)) for x in SITES}
    return lam, eps


def hand_at(gears, x):
    """(now, before, rho, W) of a hand at a clock."""
    W = iv(gears[0].teeth, x)
    now = (int(gears[0].now[x]), int(gears[1].now[x]))
    before = (int(gears[0].before[x]), int(gears[1].before[x]))
    rho = (int(gears[0].fine[x]) - mid(W), int(gears[1].fine[x]) - mid(W))
    return now, before, rho, W


def q_forms(now, before, rho, W, e):
    S = now[0] * before[1] - now[1] * before[0]
    qa = e * W * S
    qb = e * (W * S + im_conj(rho, now))
    qc = e * (W * S + Fraction(im_conj(rho, (now[0] + before[0], now[1] + before[1])), 2))
    return {"A": Fraction(qa), "B": Fraction(qb), "C": qc}


def densities(gears, eps):
    return {x: q_forms(*hand_at(gears, x), int(eps[x])) for x in SITES}


def total(q, lam, form):
    return sum((lam[x] * q[x][form] for x in SITES), Fraction(0))


ORIG_CARRY, ORIG_TURNOVER, ORIG_RUN = tickmod.Tick.carry, tickmod.Tick.turnover, tickmod.run
ORIG_CLICK = clockmod.Clock.click


def carry(self):
    if self.going != 1:
        return ORIG_CARRY(self)
    clk = self.clock
    lam, eps = weights(clk)
    X = np.asarray(self.stretch_now, dtype=object) * np.ones(size, dtype=object)
    p = np.asarray(self.mesh_rate, dtype=object) * np.ones(size, dtype=object)
    w_side = readings.mean_teeth(clk, self.regulator.gears[0])
    stopped = np.asarray(self.stopped, dtype=bool) * np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
    C("C0 lam_t read from the state = 1/(X p^2) of the tick's own readings (1 under QR3)", all(
        lam[x] == ((1 if QR3 else Fraction(1, int(X[x]) * int(p[x]) ** 2)) if int(p[x]) else 0) for x in SITES))
    C("C0b stopped clock-ticks (their weight 0: p = 0; their Meshes at the teeth)", True, int(stopped.sum()))
    pre = {}
    for k in coupled:
        r = clk.hands()[k]
        t = self.where[id(r)]
        C("C0c the start gears' teeth = w_side, the impulse's teeth", bool(np.all(self.trains[t][0].teeth == w_side)))
        a = self.starts[t]
        gearing = readings.gearing_of(clk, r, self.load, self.factors)
        rotation = coupling_of(r) * rest_rate_of(clk, r.ratio)
        turn = tickmod.skewed(self.meshes_at[1], rotation, self.flow_gears, clk.full_rate)
        ms = [clk.offset_pull([across(x, j) for x in a], j, turn) for j in range(SPOKES)]
        total_m = [sum(gearing.grips[j] * ms[j][g] for j in range(SPOKES)) for g in range(2)]
        F = [tm - (gearing.teeth + gearing.teeth - gearing.hold) * x for tm, x in zip(total_m, a)]
        C("C3a the pull rebuilt from the engine's own turned neighbours, bit for bit",
          all(bool(np.all(F[g] == self.pull[t][g])) for g in range(2)))
        Sj = [2 * self.factors[j] * r.ratio[0] for j in range(SPOKES)]
        C("C3b the grips = p^2 S_j, S_j = 2 Q_j pinion the same from both ends of the Mesh", all(
            bool(np.all(gearing.grips[j] == p * p * Sj[j])) and bool(np.all(across(Sj[j ^ 1], j) == Sj[j]))
            for j in range(SPOKES)))
        if QR3:
            Xp2 = X * p * p
            Sym = [self.factors[j] * r.ratio[0] * (Xp2 + across(Xp2, j)) for j in range(SPOKES)]
            C("C3c QR3: the coupling G_j the same from both ends of the Mesh", all(
                bool(np.all(across(Sym[j ^ 1], j) == Sym[j])) for j in range(SPOKES)))
            imp = [v.copy() for v in self.xf[t]]
        else:
            Sym = Sj
            imp = [X * f for f in self.pull[t]]
        pre[k] = dict(start=[g.copy() for g in self.trains[t]], F=[f.copy() for f in self.pull[t]], ms=ms, Sj=Sym,
                      turn=turn, imp=imp, t=t)
    caught = {}
    orig_set = clk.set_trains

    def set_trains(out):
        orig_set(out)
        caught["post"] = {k: [g.copy() for g in clk.hands()[k].gears] for k in coupled}

    clk.set_trains = set_trains
    ORIG_CARRY(self)
    del clk.set_trains
    restored = {k: [g.copy() for g in clk.hands()[k].gears] for k in coupled}
    masked = {k: getattr(self, "masked", {}).get(pre[k]["t"]) for k in coupled} if QR4 else {}
    stage.update(lam=lam, eps=eps, X=X, p=p, stopped=stopped, pre=pre, post=caught["post"], restored=restored,
                 masked=masked, predicted=getattr(self, "predicted_top", None))


def turnover(self):
    ORIG_TURNOVER(self)
    if self.going != 1:
        return
    stage["top"] = np.asarray(self.top, dtype=bool) * np.ones(size, dtype=bool)
    stage["after_turn"] = {k: [g.copy() for g in self.clock.hands()[k].gears] for k in coupled}
    stage["eps_after"] = 1 - 2 * np.asarray(ratchet_of(self.clock.trains_).gears[0].fine, dtype=object)


def click_wrapped(self, starts, held, going=1, note=None):
    if going != 1:
        return ORIG_CLICK(self, starts, held, going, note)
    lam1, eps1 = weights(self)
    before = {k: [g.copy() for g in self.hands()[k].gears] for k in coupled}
    log_from = len(self.log)
    ORIG_CLICK(self, starts, held, going, note)
    lam2, eps2 = weights(self)
    C("C9a the click moves no weight and no pawl", lam1 == lam2 and bool(np.all(eps1 == eps2)))
    after = {k: [g.copy() for g in self.hands()[k].gears] for k in coupled}
    entries = list(self.log[log_from:])
    stage["lam_next"], stage["eps_next"], stage["after_click"], stage["entries"] = lam1, eps1, after, entries
    book_click(self, before, after, entries, lam1, eps1)


def book_click(clk, before, after, entries, lam, eps):
    """The click's charge book, per form: Q* before against after (the next tick's weights, which the click does not
    move), attributed to the clocks the click's events name: a fitting's two ends, an unspringing's two ends, a credit's
    clock; and per event the two ends' framed charges (form C) and plain sweeps before the write."""
    qb = {k: densities(before[k], eps) for k in coupled}
    qa = {k: densities(after[k], eps) for k in coupled}
    d = {f: sum((lam[x] * (qa[k][x][f] - qb[k][x][f]) for k in coupled for x in SITES), Fraction(0)) for f in FORMS}

    def sw(gears, x):
        n, b = hand_at(gears, x)[:2]
        return n[0] * b[1] - n[1] * b[0]

    plain_moved = sum(sw(after[k], x) - sw(before[k], x) for k in coupled for x in SITES)
    kinds = Counter(e.get("event") for e in entries if e.get("event") != "refused")
    named = set()
    events = []
    for e in entries:
        ev = e.get("event")
        if ev == "birth":
            mi, pl = tuple(e["minus"]), tuple(e["plus"])
            unspring = "takes" in e
            h = e["taken_row"] if unspring else e["line_pair"]
            ends = (mi, pl)
        elif ev == "credit":
            h, ends, unspring = e["line"], (tuple(e["node"]),), None
        else:
            continue
        named |= {(h, x) for x in ends}
        if h not in coupled:
            events.append(dict(ev=ev, unspring=unspring, coupled=False))
            continue
        dq = sum((lam[x] * (qa[h][x]["C"] - qb[h][x]["C"]) for x in set(ends)), Fraction(0))
        info = dict(ev=ev, unspring=unspring, coupled=True, dq=dq, tick=clk.ticks)
        if ev == "birth":
            info["wall"] = int(eps[mi]) != int(eps[pl])
            info["w"] = (lam[mi] * iv(after[h][0].teeth, mi), lam[pl] * iv(after[h][0].teeth, pl))
            info["plain_before"] = (sw(before[h], mi), sw(before[h], pl))
            info["framed_before"] = (lam[mi] * qb[h][mi]["C"], lam[pl] * qb[h][pl]["C"])
            info["plain_after"] = (sw(after[h], mi), sw(after[h], pl))
        events.append(info)
    rest = {f: sum((lam[x] * (qa[k][x][f] - qb[k][x][f]) for k in coupled for x in SITES if (k, x) not in named),
                   Fraction(0)) for f in FORMS}
    clicks_book.append(dict(tick=clk.ticks, d=d, kinds=kinds, events=events, plain=plain_moved, rest=rest))


def run(clk, going):
    ORIG_RUN(clk, going)
    if going == 1:
        rows.append(analyse(dict(stage)))


tickmod.Tick.carry, tickmod.Tick.turnover, tickmod.run = carry, turnover, run
clockmod.Clock.click = click_wrapped


def analyse(st):
    lam, eps, X, p, stopped = st["lam"], st["eps"], st["X"], st["p"], st["stopped"]
    top, eps_a, lam_n, eps_n = st["top"], st["eps_after"], st["lam_next"], st["eps_next"]
    out = {"turned": int(top.sum()), "walls": 0, "hands": {}}
    turned = [x for x in SITES if top[x]]
    ratios = [Fraction(int(X[x]) * int(p[x]) ** 2, GAMMA ** 3) for x in SITES if int(p[x])]
    out["xp2"] = (min(ratios), max(ratios))
    out["X"] = (min(int(X[x]) for x in SITES), max(int(X[x]) for x in SITES))
    for k in coupled:
        pr = st["pre"][k]
        start, Fp, ms, Sj, turn, imp = pr["start"], pr["F"], pr["ms"], pr["Sj"], pr["turn"], pr["imp"]
        taken = st["masked"].get(k) if QR4 else None
        if taken is not None:
            imp = [i - t_ for i, t_ in zip(imp, taken)]
        if QR4:
            C("C7f QR4: the turned clocks read before the hands' carry = the turnover's own", bool(np.all(
                np.asarray(st["predicted"], dtype=bool) == top)))
        post, rest, aft, clk_ = st["post"][k], st["restored"][k], st["after_turn"][k], st["after_click"][k]
        q0 = densities(start, eps)
        q1 = {x: (q_forms(*hand_at(rest, x), int(eps[x])) if stopped[x] else q_forms(*hand_at(post, x), int(eps[x])))
              for x in SITES}
        carry_t = {f: {} for f in FORMS}
        ok = {f: True for f in FORMS}
        ok3 = True
        okm1 = True
        for x in SITES:
            a, am, rho, ws = hand_at(start, x)
            ap, _a2, rhop, wd = hand_at(post, x)
            v, vp = (a[0] - am[0], a[1] - am[1]), (ap[0] - a[0], ap[1] - a[1])
            e = int(eps[x])
            carry_t["A"][x] = e * (im_conj(rho, a) - im_conj(rhop, a))
            carry_t["B"][x] = -e * im_conj(vp, rhop)
            carry_t["C"][x] = -e * Fraction(im_conj(vp, rhop) + im_conj(v, rho), 2)
            if stopped[x]:
                continue
            ix = (int(imp[0][x]), int(imp[1][x]))
            flux_term = -e * im_conj(a, ix)
            for f in FORMS:
                ok[f] &= q1[x][f] - q0[x][f] == flux_term + carry_t[f][x]
            okm1 &= q1[x]["C"] - q0[x]["C"] == flux_term
            scale = 1 if QR3 else int(X[x]) * int(p[x]) ** 2
            ok3 &= im_conj(a, ix) == scale * sum(
                int(Sj[j][x]) * im_conj(a, (int(ms[j][0][x]), int(ms[j][1][x]))) for j in range(SPOKES)
                if not (QR4 and not top[x] and top[nb(x, j)]))
        for f in FORMS:
            C(f"C1{f} the step's continuity, form {f}, at every live clock, per hand-tick", ok[f])
        C("C3 Im(conj a X F) = SUM_j c_j Im(conj a m_j), c_j = X p^2 S_j (head) or G_j (QR3), per hand-tick", ok3)
        MU("M1 the continuity of form C without Rule3's torque (per hand-tick)", okm1)
        # ---- the Meshes' flux
        I = {}
        for x in SITES:
            a = hand_at(start, x)[0]
            for j in range(SPOKES):
                if QR4 and not top[x] and top[nb(x, j)]:
                    I[(x, j)] = 0
                    continue
                I[(x, j)] = int(eps[x]) * int(Sj[j][x]) * im_conj(a, (int(ms[j][0][x]), int(ms[j][1][x])))
        sumT, pawl_part, ok5 = Fraction(0), Fraction(0), True
        sumT_inner = sumT_nb = Fraction(0)
        T_of = {}
        for x in SITES:
            ax = hand_at(start, x)[0]
            for axis in range(AXES):
                j = spoke_of(axis, 0)
                y = nb(x, j)
                ay = hand_at(start, y)[0]
                Tm = I[(x, j)] + I[(y, j + 1)]
                T_of[(x, j)] = Tm
                sumT += Tm
                mesh = turn[j]
                f = iv(mesh.pawl, x)
                ident = iv(mesh.odd, x) == 0 and iv(mesh.skew[1], x) == 0
                zero_n = (iv(mesh.cosine, x), iv(mesh.sine, x)) == (int(clock.circuit[0][0]), 0)
                if f < 0 and k == coupled[0]:
                    out["walls"] += 1
                exact = Fraction(0)
                boundary_mesh = QR4 and bool(top[x]) != bool(top[y])
                for (u, w_, jj) in ((x, y, j), (y, x, j + 1)):
                    m2 = turn[jj]
                    if QR4 and not top[u] and top[w_]:
                        continue
                    w2 = m2.skew[0]
                    od, mm = iv(m2.odd, u), iv(m2.skew[1], u)
                    g3, g12 = WL * w2 - od * mm, WL * mm + od * w2
                    nn = g3 * g3 + g12 * g12
                    o = (Fraction(iv(m2.cosine, u), L), Fraction(iv(m2.sine, u), L))
                    t = (Fraction(g3 * g3 - g12 * g12, nn), Fraction(2 * g3 * g12, nn))
                    zu, zw = hand_at(start, u)[0], hand_at(start, w_)[0]
                    zw = zw if iv(m2.pawl, u) == 1 else gconj(zw)
                    exact += int(eps[u]) * int(Sj[jj][u]) * im_conj(zu, gmul(gmul(o, t), zw))
                    if u == x:
                        o_x, t_x = o, t
                formula = (2 * int(eps[x]) * int(Sj[j][x]) * t_x[1] * re_conj((1, 0), gmul(o_x, gconj(gmul(ax, ay))))
                           if f < 0 and not TRIPLE and not boundary_mesh else Fraction(0))
                if not boundary_mesh:
                    ok5 &= exact == formula
                    pawl_part += exact
                    sumT_nb += Tm
                key = ("beside a turn" if boundary_mesh else "pawl wall" if f < 0 else "one frame",
                       "identity triple" if ident else "odd/skew turn", "count 0" if zero_n else "count != 0")
                if not top[x] and not top[y]:
                    sumT_inner += Tm
                for kk, val in (((k,) + key, Tm), ((k, "rounding part") + key[1:], Tm - exact)):
                    c4 = cls[kk]
                    c4[0] += 1
                    c4[1] += val == 0
                    c4[2] += abs(val) * VSU
                    c4[3] = max(c4[3], abs(val) * VSU)
        C("C5 the exact transport's defect = 2 eps S Im(t) Re(o conj(a_x a_y)) on walls (head), 0 elsewhere", ok5)
        live = [x for x in SITES if not stopped[x]]
        res = {}
        qa = densities(aft, eps_a)
        qc = densities(clk_, eps_n)
        for f in FORMS:
            Q0, Q1 = total(q0, lam, f), total(q1, lam, f)
            carry_term = sum((lam[x] * carry_t[f][x] for x in live), Fraction(0))
            C(f"C6{f} the step's book, form {f}: dQ*_step = -SUM_Meshes T + SUM lam carry, exactly", Q1 - Q0 == -sumT + carry_term)
            if f == "C":
                noeps = sum((I[(x, j)] * int(eps[x]) for x in SITES for j in range(SPOKES)), Fraction(0))
                MU("M2 the step's book with the flux read with no pawl sign (eps dropped)", Q1 - Q0 == -noeps + carry_term)
            Q2 = total(qa, lam, f)
            own = sum((lam[x] * (qa[x][f] - q0[x][f]) for x in turned), Fraction(0))
            discarded = sum((I[(x, j)] for x in turned for j in range(SPOKES)), Fraction(0)) - sum(
                (lam[x] * carry_t[f][x] for x in turned), Fraction(0))
            C(f"C7{f} the turnover's book, form {f}: dQ*_turn = SUM_turned lam (q' - q_start) + the discarded step",
              Q2 - Q1 == own + discarded)
            Q3 = total(qa, lam_n, f)
            Q4 = total(qc, lam_n, f)
            if QR4:
                unturned_carry = sum((lam[x] * carry_t[f][x] for x in live if not top[x]), Fraction(0))
                C(f"C7g{f} QR4: step + turnover = -SUM_(Meshes of unturned ends) T + SUM_unturned lam carry + SUM_turned lam (q' - q)",
                  Q2 - Q0 == -sumT_inner + unturned_carry + own)
            res[f] = dict(Q0=Q0, step=Q1 - Q0, carry=carry_term, turn=Q2 - Q1, own=own, discarded=discarded, step_turn=Q2 - Q0,
                          bg=Q3 - Q2, bg_turned=sum(((lam_n[x] - lam[x]) * qa[x][f] for x in turned), Fraction(0)),
                          click=Q4 - Q3, Q4=Q4)
        sign = 1 if TURN else -1
        if turned:
            MU("M3 at every turned clock form C turned the other way (q' = %s q)" % ("-" if TURN else "+"),
               all(qa[x]["C"] == -sign * q0[x]["C"] for x in turned))
        for f in ("A", "C"):
            C(f"C7a{f} at every turned clock, form {f}: q' = {'+' if TURN else '-'} q of the tick's start",
              all(qa[x][f] == sign * q0[x][f] for x in turned), len(turned))
            edge = [x for x in turned if qa[x][f] != sign * q0[x][f]]
            if f == "C" and edge:
                C("C7e form C's misses at turned clocks all at the even-teeth edge (a fine tooth 0)", all(
                    0 in (int(start[0].fine[x]), int(start[1].fine[x])) for x in edge), len(edge))
        C("C7b the turnover moves no unturned clock's density", all(qa[x] == q1[x] for x in SITES if not top[x]))
        boundary = sum((I[(x, j)] for x in turned for j in range(SPOKES) if not top[nb(x, j)]), Fraction(0))
        internal = sum((T_of[(x, j)] for x in turned for j in (0, 2, 4) if top[nb(x, j)]), Fraction(0))
        C("C7d the discarded step's flux = the flux out of the turned region + its inner Meshes' defects",
          sum((I[(x, j)] for x in turned for j in range(SPOKES)), Fraction(0)) == boundary + internal)
        out["hands"][k] = dict(res=res, T=-sumT_nb, pawl=-pawl_part, rnd=-(sumT_nb - pawl_part), boundary=boundary)
    return out


for _ in range(ticks):
    clock.tick()

for prev, nxt in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
    for k in coupled:
        for f in FORMS:
            C(f"C10{f} the chain, form {f}: Q*(start t+1) = Q*(start t) + step + turn + bg + click",
              nxt["hands"][k]["res"][f]["Q0"] == prev["hands"][k]["res"][f]["Q4"])

name = (f"{world} {ticks} ticks" + (" +QR1 the triple through the pawl" if TRIPLE else "")
        + (" +QR2 the turnover turning the sweep" if TURN else "") + (" +QR3 the Mesh-symmetric coupling, unit weight" if QR3 else "")
        + (" +QR4 the turned clocks' Meshes at the teeth" if QR4 else ""))
print(f"== c2: {name}; e = {E} VSU; coupled hands {coupled}")
print(f"  ticks with turned clocks {sum(1 for r in rows if r['turned'])} of {len(rows)}, turned clock-ticks {sum(r['turned'] for r in rows)};"
      f" pawl walls {sum(r['walls'] for r in rows)} of {len(rows) * len(SITES) * AXES} Mesh-ticks")
C.report()
print("  mutants (each must show failures where the world holds charge and walls):")
MU.report()
print(f"  the weights: X p^2 / Gamma^3 over the clocks, least {float(min(r['xp2'][0] for r in rows)):.6f}, largest"
      f" {float(max(r['xp2'][1] for r in rows)):.6f} (1 at the vacuum balance X p0^4 = Gamma^5, p = p0^2 / Gamma);"
      f" the stretch X / Gamma from {min(r['X'][0] for r in rows) / GAMMA:.3f} to {max(r['X'][1] for r in rows) / GAMMA:.3f}")
print("  the Mesh defect T = I_j(x) + I_-j(x+e_j) by class (Mesh-ticks, T == 0, SUM |T|, max |T| in VSU):")
for key in sorted(cls, key=str):
    n, z, s, m = cls[key]
    if n:
        print(f"    hand {key[0]} {key[1]:13s} {key[2]:15s} {key[3]:10s}: {n:6d}, exact 0 at {z:6d}, SUM {float(s):14.3f}, max {float(m):12.3f}")
print("  per hand: the Mesh terms of dQ*_step (the same for every form): largest |.| (VSU), ticks nonzero, summed")
for k in coupled:
    for part in ("T", "pawl", "rnd", "boundary"):
        vals = [r["hands"][k][part] for r in rows]
        print(f"   hand {k} {part:9s} largest {float(max(abs(v) for v in vals) * VSU):14.3f}  nonzero {sum(1 for v in vals if v):3d}"
              f" of {len(vals)}  summed {float(sum(vals) * VSU):14.3f}")
print("  per hand and form, per act: largest |dQ*| (VSU), ticks nonzero, summed (VSU)")
for k in coupled:
    for f in FORMS:
        print(f"   hand {k} form {f}: Q* {float(rows[0]['hands'][k]['res'][f]['Q0'] * VSU):.1f} -> {float(rows[-1]['hands'][k]['res'][f]['Q4'] * VSU):.1f}")
        for part in ("step", "carry", "turn", "own", "discarded", "step_turn", "bg", "bg_turned", "click"):
            vals = [r["hands"][k]["res"][f][part] for r in rows]
            print(f"     {part:10s} largest {float(max(abs(v) for v in vals) * VSU):14.3f}  nonzero {sum(1 for v in vals if v):3d}"
                  f" of {len(vals)}  summed {float(sum(vals) * VSU):14.3f}")
print("  the clicks' book (Q* before against after every click; the next tick's weights; VSU):")
n_click = sum(1 for cb in clicks_book if cb["kinds"])
kept = {f: sum(1 for cb in clicks_book if cb["kinds"] and cb["d"][f] == 0) for f in FORMS}
print(f"   clicks with an event: {n_click}; Q* kept exactly: A {kept['A']}, B {kept['B']}, C {kept['C']};"
      f" Q* moved off the events' clocks: {sum(1 for cb in clicks_book if any(cb['rest'].values()))} clicks")
evs = [e for cb in clicks_book for e in cb["events"]]
for label, pick in (("fitting", lambda e: e["ev"] == "birth" and not e["unspring"]),
                    ("unspringing", lambda e: e["ev"] == "birth" and e["unspring"]),
                    ("credit", lambda e: e["ev"] == "credit")):
    es = [e for e in evs if pick(e)]
    ec = [e for e in es if e["coupled"]]
    keptC = sum(1 for e in ec if e["dq"] == 0)
    big = max((abs(e["dq"]) * VSU for e in ec), default=0)
    line = f"   {label:11s}: {len(es):4d}, on a coupled hand {len(ec):4d}; Q* kept at {keptC}; largest |dQ*| {float(big):12.1f}"
    if label == "fitting" and ec:
        pf = max(abs(E * (e["w"][1] - e["w"][0]) * VSU) for e in ec)
        line += f"; with the senses in the pawl frame, largest |dQ*| {float(pf):.1f} (the weights' part alone)"
    if label == "unspringing" and ec:
        same = sum(1 for e in ec if e["framed_before"][0] * e["framed_before"][1] > 0)
        line += f"; pairs of one framed sign unwound {same}"
    if label != "credit" and ec:
        line += f"; across a pawl wall {sum(1 for e in ec if e['wall'])}; ends' weights lam W unequal {sum(1 for e in ec if e['w'][0] != e['w'][1])}"
    print(line)
    if label == "unspringing":
        for e in ec:
            pb, fb = e["plain_before"], e["framed_before"]
            print(f"     tick {e['tick']:3d}: plain sweeps before {pb} (opposite: {pb[0] * pb[1] < 0}); framed weighted charges before"
                  f" ({float(fb[0] * VSU):.1f}, {float(fb[1] * VSU):.1f}) (opposite: {fb[0] * fb[1] < 0}, summing to {float((fb[0] + fb[1]) * VSU):.1f});"
                  f" across a wall {e['wall']}; dQ* {float(e['dq'] * VSU):.1f}")
    if label == "fitting":
        for e in ec[:12]:
            w0, w1 = e["w"]
            print(f"     tick {e['tick']:3d}: wall {e['wall']}; the ends' weights lam W in the vacuum's (1 there) {float(w0 * VSU):.4f}, {float(w1 * VSU):.4f};"
                  f" dQ* {float(e['dq'] * VSU):.1f} (e = {E}); with the senses in the pawl frame {float(E * (w1 - w0) * VSU):.1f};"
                  f" plain sweeps after {e['plain_after']}")

== c3_rewind.py ==
"""c3: the rewrites keep the rewind. The head's engine, and the engine with QR1 (the odd gear and the skew summed
through the pawl), QR2 (the turnover exchanges a hand's slots with no K, its stored sweep turned with the pawl) and
both, each run N ticks forward and N back; every integer of every train (both notches, the fine tooth, the drive's
fine tooth, the teeth), the slack, the dial and the clicks compared;
QR3 (the Mesh's coupling the same from both ends) and QR4 (the turned clocks' Meshes at the teeth for the tick of
their turnover; rewrites.py) likewise, with the start, bit for bit; and the runs' own
counts (turned clock-ticks, births, unspringings) so that the rewrites are seen to act.  A mutant that must fail:
QR2 spelt as a turnover that is no involution (the twelve gear negated on the way out only), and QR4 read
forward only (the backward impulse not given back the Mesh terms the forward took out).
Run: venv314/bin/python -I c3_rewind.py world ticks
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/charge"
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from common import WORLDS, engine  # noqa: E402

engine()
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

from event_universe import clock as clockmod  # noqa: E402
from event_universe import hands  # noqa: E402
from event_universe import tick as tickmod  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.assembly.piece import read_piece  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core import acts  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.core.spokes import AXES, SPOKE_SIDES, across  # noqa: E402
from event_universe.meshes import Mesh  # noqa: E402

world, ticks = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2])
import rewrites  # noqa: E402

ORIG_REVERSE = acts.reverse


def broken_reverse(gears, signs):  # the mutant: the twelve gear negated on the way out only, no involution
    nows, befores, fines = ORIG_REVERSE(gears, signs)
    if len(gears) == 2:
        nows = [nows[0], -nows[1]]
    return nows, befores, fines


def state(clock):
    out = []
    for r in clock.trains_:
        for g in r.gears:
            out.append(tuple(np.asarray(v, dtype=object).copy() if v is not None else None
                             for v in (g.now, g.before, g.fine, g.teeth, g.dfine)))
    out.append((clock.slack.copy(), clock.dial.copy(), clock.clicks.copy()))
    return out


def same(s1, s2):
    for a, b in zip(s1, s2, strict=True):
        for u, v in zip(a, b, strict=True):
            if (u is None) != (v is None):
                return False
            if u is not None and not bool(np.all(np.asarray(u) == np.asarray(v))):
                return False
    return True


def run(label, triple, turn, r3=False, mutant=False, r4=False, mutant4=False):
    rewrites.apply(triple, turn, r3, r4)
    acts.reverse = broken_reverse if mutant else ORIG_REVERSE
    if mutant4:  # QR4 read forward only: the backward impulse not given back the terms the forward took out
        tickmod.Tick.impulse = rewrites.IMPULSE_R3
    calibre, piece = read_piece(Path(WORLDS) / world)
    clock = clockmod.Clock(calibre, piece)
    s0 = state(clock)
    for _ in range(ticks):
        clock.tick()
    turned = sum(n for _t, n in clock.turnovers)
    births = clock.counts["births"]
    unspr = sum(1 for e in clock.log if e.get("event") == "birth" and "takes" in e)
    sN = state(clock)
    try:
        for _ in range(ticks):
            clock.tick(-1)
        ok = same(s0, state(clock))
    except Exception as exc:  # a mutant may break the rewind by an error as well as by a difference
        ok = False
        print(f"    ({label}: the rewind raised {type(exc).__name__})")
    moved = not same(s0, sN)
    print(f"  {label:40s}: {ticks} ticks forward, {ticks} back: start state returned bit for bit: {ok};"
          f" turned clock-ticks {turned}, births {births} (unspringings {unspr}); the run moved the state: {moved}")
    return ok


print(f"== c3: the rewind with and without the rewrites, {world}")
oks = [run("head", False, False), run("QR1 the triple through the pawl", True, False),
       run("QR2 the turnover turning the sweep", False, True), run("QR1 + QR2", True, True),
       run("QR3 the Mesh-symmetric coupling", False, False, True), run("QR1 + QR2 + QR3", True, True, True),
       run("QR4 the turned clocks' Meshes at the teeth", False, False, r4=True),
       run("QR1 + QR2 + QR3 + QR4", True, True, True, r4=True)]
bad = run("mutant: QR2 spelt with no involution", False, True, mutant=True)
acts.reverse = ORIG_REVERSE
bad4 = run("mutant: QR4 read forward only", False, False, r4=True, mutant4=True)
print(f"  every variant rewinds: {all(oks)}; the mutants fail to rewind: {not bad and not bad4}")

== c4_toy.py ==
"""c4: the one formula's letter in exact rationals on a 3-D torus (no carry, no rounding), many ticks: which charge is
kept, and what each rewrite buys.  The step at every clock x (the head's letter with the remainder 0):
    P = W (a - a-),  P+ = P + X F,  a+ = a + P+ / W+,
    F(x) = SUM_j g_j(x) (U_j(x) K^f a(x + e_j) - a(x)) - D a(x),  g_j(x) = p(x)^2 S_j (S_j the same from both ends),
U the Mesh's exact turn (the offset's pair at the count n = K_x - f K_y, the odd gear's and the skew's tangent triple,
summed with no pawl on the head, through the pawl under QR1), K^f the pawl's reflection.  Q*_t = SUM_x lam_t eps W S,
lam_t = 1/(X_t p_t^2) at the tick's start, S = Im(conj a a-).
  E1  fixed rates: Q* kept to the last digit by the step with QR1, over every tick; with the head's triple it moves.
  E2  rates moving in time (X_t(x) p_t(x)^2 not of one shape): the step keeps Q*_t within each tick (with that tick's
      weights) and the chain across ticks breaks by exactly SUM (lam_t+1 - lam_t) eps W S; with QR3, the Mesh's
      coupling read the same from both ends with the stretch inside, G_xy = (X_x p_x^2 + X_y p_y^2) / 2 S_xy, the
      unit-weight charge Q = SUM eps W S is kept across every tick.
  E3  split turnovers (a turned clock ends the tick at its pre-state reversed, its step discarded): the head's
      reversal (K and the pawl flipped) reverses eps W S at the turned clock; QR2 (no K, the pawl flipped) keeps it,
      and Q* then moves by exactly the discarded step's flux out of the turned region; with the turned clocks'
      Meshes at the teeth for the tick (the candidate, the turned set known before the step) Q* is kept exactly.
  E4  births: a pair (sigma A, i B) at the two ends of a Mesh on zero gears, F1's senses (sigma_y = -sigma_x) against
      the senses in the pawl frame (sigma_y = -f sigma_x): Q* moved by e (eps_x lam_x W_x + ... ) on a wall, and by
      e (lam_x W_x - lam_y W_y) on a frame Mesh whose two ends' weights differ.
Mutants that must fail are counted beside each claim.  Run: venv314/bin/python -I c4_toy.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import itertools
import random
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as Fr

HERE = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-Universe24/2ac4c1db-b416-55d7-b373-04f98f505363/scratchpad/derive/charge"
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from common import Tally, gconj, gmul, im_conj  # noqa: E402

rng = random.Random(4096)
T = Tally()
M = Tally()
L = 3
SITES = list(itertools.product(range(L), repeat=3))
SPOKES = [(a, s) for a in range(3) for s in (1, -1)]
WL, W2 = 4000, 10 ** 6


def nb(x, j):
    a, s = SPOKES[j]
    y = list(x)
    y[a] = (y[a] + s) % L
    return tuple(y)


def tangent(base, n):
    return (Fr(base * base - n * n, base * base + n * n), Fr(2 * base * n, base * base + n * n))


def triple(odd, m):
    g3, g12 = WL * W2 - odd * m, WL * m + odd * W2
    nn = g3 * g3 + g12 * g12
    return (Fr(g3 * g3 - g12 * g12, nn), Fr(2 * g3 * g12, nn))


def world():
    S = {}
    for x in SITES:
        for j, (a, s) in enumerate(SPOKES):
            if s == 1:
                S[(x, j)] = S[(nb(x, j), j + 1)] = rng.randrange(1, 6)
    return dict(S=S, K={x: rng.randrange(-5, 6) for x in SITES}, A={x: [rng.randrange(-6, 7) for _ in range(3)] for x in SITES},
                V={x: [rng.randrange(-6, 7) for _ in range(3)] for x in SITES}, D={x: rng.randrange(0, 30) for x in SITES},
                bits={x: rng.randint(0, 1) for x in SITES}, base=rng.randrange(40, 400), Kr=rng.randrange(1, 900))


def links(wd, rule, bits):
    eps = {x: 1 - 2 * bits[x] for x in SITES}
    out = {}
    for x in SITES:
        for j, (a, s) in enumerate(SPOKES):
            y = nb(x, j)
            f = eps[x] * eps[y]
            o = tangent(wd["base"], wd["K"][x] - f * wd["K"][y])
            if rule == "head":
                t = triple(s * (wd["A"][x][a] + wd["A"][y][a]), 3 * wd["Kr"] * s * (wd["V"][x][a] + wd["V"][y][a]))
            else:
                t = triple(s * (wd["A"][x][a] + f * wd["A"][y][a]), 3 * wd["Kr"] * s * (wd["V"][x][a] + f * wd["V"][y][a]))
            out[(x, j)] = (y, f, gmul(o, t))
    return out, eps


def force(wd, lk, a, X, p, sym=False, off=frozenset()):
    """X F at every clock: the head's form X(x) p(x)^2 S on every Mesh, or QR3's Mesh-symmetric G (sym); a Mesh
    touching a clock of `off` at the teeth (its coupling 0) for the tick."""
    out = {}
    for x in SITES:
        acc = (Fr(0), Fr(0))
        for j in range(6):
            y, f, u = lk[(x, j)]
            if x in off or y in off:
                continue
            ay = a[y] if f == 1 else gconj(a[y])
            m = gmul(u, ay)
            g = (X[x] * p[x] ** 2 + X[y] * p[y] ** 2) / 2 * wd["S"][(x, j)] if sym else X[x] * p[x] ** 2 * wd["S"][(x, j)]
            acc = (acc[0] + g * (m[0] - a[x][0]), acc[1] + g * (m[1] - a[x][1]))
        out[x] = (acc[0] - X[x] * wd["D"][x] * a[x][0], acc[1] - X[x] * wd["D"][x] * a[x][1])
    return out


def sweep(n, b):
    return n[0] * b[1] - n[1] * b[0]


def qstar(a, am, W, lam, eps):
    return sum((lam[x] * eps[x] * W[x] * sweep(a[x], am[x]) for x in SITES), Fr(0))


def step(wd, lk, a, am, W, Wp, X, p, sym=False, off=frozenset()):
    XF = force(wd, lk, a, X, p, sym, off)
    new = {}
    for x in SITES:
        P = (W[x] * (a[x][0] - am[x][0]) + XF[x][0], W[x] * (a[x][1] - am[x][1]) + XF[x][1])
        new[x] = (a[x][0] + P[0] / Wp[x], a[x][1] + P[1] / Wp[x])
    return new


def step_pair(wd, lk, a, am, W, X, p):
    return step(wd, lk, a, am, W, W, X, p), a


def fresh():
    a = {x: (Fr(rng.randrange(-60, 61)), Fr(rng.randrange(-60, 61))) for x in SITES}
    am = {x: (a[x][0] + rng.randrange(-8, 9), a[x][1] + rng.randrange(-8, 9)) for x in SITES}
    return a, am


TICKS = 14
# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- E1: fixed rates
for trial in range(6):
    wd = world()
    X = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(500, 1500)) for x in SITES}
    p = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(300, 1001)) for x in SITES}
    W = {x: 10 ** 6 * X[x] for x in SITES}
    lam = {x: 1 / (X[x] * p[x] ** 2) for x in SITES}
    for rule in ("rewrite", "head"):
        lk, eps = links(wd, rule, wd["bits"])
        a, am = fresh()
        Q0 = qstar(a, am, W, lam, eps)
        moved = 0
        for _t in range(TICKS):
            a, am = step(wd, lk, a, am, W, W, X, p), a
            moved += qstar(a, am, W, lam, eps) != Q0
        if rule == "rewrite":
            T("E1 fixed rates, pawl walls, QR1: Q* kept to the last digit, tick by tick", moved == 0, TICKS)
            T("E1b at rates fixed in time W = T X, so Q* = T SUM eps S / p^2, the law's weight 1/p^2 with the pawl's sign",
              qstar(a, am, W, lam, eps) == 10 ** 6 * sum((eps[x] * sweep(a[x], am[x]) / p[x] ** 2 for x in SITES), Fr(0)))
            # mutants: the law's weight with no pawl sign; the stride's form weighed by 1/p^2 alone (the 1/X dropped)
            a, am = fresh()
            law0 = sum((sweep(a[x], am[x]) / p[x] ** 2 for x in SITES), Fr(0))
            Pform = lambda a_, am_: sum((eps[x] * im_conj(a_[x], (W[x] * (a_[x][0] - am_[x][0]), W[x] * (a_[x][1] - am_[x][1])))
                                         / p[x] ** 2 for x in SITES), Fr(0))
            pf0 = Pform(a, am)
            mv_law = mv_pf = 0
            for _t in range(TICKS):
                a, am = step(wd, lk, a, am, W, W, X, p), a
                mv_law += sum((sweep(a[x], am[x]) / p[x] ** 2 for x in SITES), Fr(0)) != law0
                mv_pf += Pform(a, am) != pf0
            M("E1-m2 the law's weight with no pawl sign, SUM S / p^2, kept", mv_law == 0, TICKS)
            M("E1-m3 the stride's form weighed by 1/p^2 alone (no 1/X), SUM eps Im(conj a P) / p^2, kept", mv_pf == 0, TICKS)
            # mutant: the plain sweep (no weight, no pawl) is not kept
            lk2, eps2 = links(wd, rule, wd["bits"])
            a, am = fresh()
            S0 = sum(sweep(a[x], am[x]) for x in SITES)
            mv = 0
            for _t in range(TICKS):
                a, am = step(wd, lk2, a, am, W, W, X, p), a
                mv += sum(sweep(a[x], am[x]) for x in SITES) != S0
            M("E1-m1 the plain sweep SUM S is kept (no pawl, no weight)", mv == 0, TICKS)
        else:
            T("E1h fixed rates, pawl walls, the head's triple: Q* moves (ticks moved counted as failures)", moved == 0, TICKS)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- E2: moving rates
for trial in range(6):
    wd = world()
    lk, eps = links(wd, "rewrite", wd["bits"])
    X0 = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(800, 1200)) for x in SITES}
    dX = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(-40, 41)) for x in SITES}
    p0 = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(500, 1001)) for x in SITES}
    dp = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(-9, 10)) for x in SITES}

    def Xp(t):
        return {x: X0[x] + t * dX[x] for x in SITES}, {x: p0[x] + t * dp[x] for x in SITES}

    def Wt(t):  # the stride's teeth, the mean of the stretch's two notches, T (X_t-1 + X_t) / 2
        Xa, _ = Xp(t - 1)
        Xb, _ = Xp(t)
        return {x: 10 ** 6 * (Xa[x] + Xb[x]) / 2 for x in SITES}

    for sym in (False, True):
        a, am = fresh()
        within = across = 0
        bg_ok = True
        for t in range(1, TICKS + 1):
            X, p = Xp(t)
            W, Wp = Wt(t), Wt(t + 1)
            lam = {x: 1 / (X[x] * p[x] ** 2) for x in SITES}
            one = {x: Fr(1) for x in SITES}
            wt = one if sym else lam
            Qs = qstar(a, am, W, wt, eps)
            a2 = step(wd, lk, a, am, W, Wp, X, p, sym=sym)
            Qe = qstar(a2, a, Wp, wt, eps)
            within += Qe != Qs
            Xn, pn = Xp(t + 1)
            lamn = one if sym else {x: 1 / (Xn[x] * pn[x] ** 2) for x in SITES}
            Qn = qstar(a2, a, Wp, lamn, eps)
            across += Qn != Qs
            bg_ok &= Qn - Qe == sum(((lamn[x] - wt[x]) * eps[x] * Wp[x] * sweep(a2[x], a[x]) for x in SITES), Fr(0))
            a, am = a2, a
        if sym:
            T("E2 QR3 (G_xy = (X_x p_x^2 + X_y p_y^2)/2 S_xy): the unit-weight Q = SUM eps W S kept within every tick", within == 0, TICKS)
            T("E2 QR3: and across every tick, the rates moving", across == 0, TICKS)
        else:
            T("E2 the head's coupling, rates moving: Q*_t kept within each tick with that tick's weights", within == 0, TICKS)
            T("E2h the head's coupling, rates moving: the chain across ticks breaks (counted as failures)", across == 0, TICKS)
            T("E2b the chain's break is exactly SUM (lam_t+1 - lam_t) eps W S", bg_ok, TICKS)
    # mutant: QR3 with the unit weight but the head's coupling (the asymmetric X(x) p(x)^2) is not kept
    a, am = fresh()
    mv = 0
    for t in range(1, TICKS + 1):
        X, p = Xp(t)
        W, Wp = Wt(t), Wt(t + 1)
        one = {x: Fr(1) for x in SITES}
        Qs = qstar(a, am, W, one, eps)
        a2 = step(wd, lk, a, am, W, Wp, X, p, sym=False)
        mv += qstar(a2, a, Wp, one, eps) != Qs
        a, am = a2, a
    M("E2-m1 the unit weight with the head's coupling X(x) p(x)^2 S: kept", mv == 0, TICKS)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- E3: split turnovers
for trial in range(8):
    wd = world()
    X = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(500, 1500)) for x in SITES}
    p = {x: Fr(rng.randrange(300, 1001)) for x in SITES}
    W = {x: 10 ** 6 * X[x] for x in SITES}
    lam = {x: 1 / (X[x] * p[x] ** 2) for x in SITES}
    for mode in ("head", "QR2", "QR2+teeth"):
        bits = dict(wd["bits"])
        a, am = fresh()
        lk, eps = links(wd, "rewrite", bits)
        Q = qstar(a, am, W, lam, eps)
        kept_own = kept_total = exact_book = 0
        n_turns = 0
        for t in range(TICKS):
            turned = frozenset(x for x in SITES if rng.random() < 0.25) if t % 3 == 2 else frozenset()
            off = turned if mode == "QR2+teeth" else frozenset()
            a2 = step(wd, lk, a, am, W, W, X, p, off=off)
            if turned:
                n_turns += 1
                XF = force(wd, lk, a, X, p, off=off)
                # the flux out of each turned clock through its Meshes, read at the step's start
                outflow = Fr(0)
                for x in turned:
                    for j in range(6):
                        y, f, u = lk[(x, j)]
                        if x in off or y in off:
                            continue
                        ay = a[y] if f == 1 else gconj(a[y])
                        outflow += eps[x] * wd["S"][(x, j)] * im_conj(a[x], gmul(u, ay))
                own_ok = True
                new_a, new_am = dict(a2), dict(a)
                for x in turned:
                    q_pre = eps[x] * W[x] * sweep(a[x], am[x])
                    if mode == "head":
                        new_a[x], new_am[x] = gconj(am[x]), gconj(a[x])
                    else:
                        new_a[x], new_am[x] = am[x], a[x]
                    bits[x] = 1 - bits[x]
                lk, eps = links(wd, "rewrite", bits)
                for x in turned:
                    q_after = eps[x] * W[x] * sweep(new_a[x], new_am[x])
                    q_pre = -eps[x] * W[x] * sweep(a[x], am[x])  # eps flipped: the pre-state's framed charge
                    own_ok &= q_after == (-q_pre if mode == "head" else q_pre)
                if mode == "head":
                    T("E3 head: at every turned clock eps W S reversed", own_ok)
                else:
                    T(f"E3 {mode}: at every turned clock eps W S kept", own_ok)
                Qn = qstar(new_a, new_am, W, lam, eps)
                if mode == "QR2":
                    exact_book += Qn - Q == outflow
                if mode == "QR2+teeth":
                    kept_total += Qn == Q
                a, am, Q = new_a, new_am, Qn
            else:
                Qn = qstar(a2, a, W, lam, eps)
                T(f"E3 {mode}: Q* kept by the step on a tick with no turnover (QR1)", Qn == Q)
                a, am, Q = a2, a, Qn
        if mode == "QR2":
            T("E3 QR2: at a split top Q* moves by exactly the discarded step's flux out of the turned clocks", exact_book == n_turns, n_turns)
        if mode == "QR2+teeth":
            T("E3 QR2 + the turned clocks' Meshes at the teeth for the tick: Q* kept at every split top", kept_total == n_turns, n_turns)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- E4: births at a Mesh
AB = 18000
moved_f1 = moved_pf = n_wall = n_frame = wall_left = 0
for _ in range(20000):
    bx, by = rng.randint(0, 1), rng.randint(0, 1)
    ex, ey = 1 - 2 * bx, 1 - 2 * by
    f = ex * ey
    lwx = Fr(rng.randrange(900, 1100), 1000)
    lwy = lwx if rng.random() < 0.5 else Fr(rng.randrange(900, 1100), 1000)
    sx = rng.choice((1, -1))
    f1 = lwx * ex * sx * AB + lwy * ey * (-sx) * AB
    pf = lwx * ex * sx * AB + lwy * ey * (-f * sx) * AB
    if f == -1:
        n_wall += 1
        T("E4 F1's senses on a wall: the pair's Q* = sigma_x e eps_x (lam_x W_x + lam_y W_y)", f1 == sx * AB * ex * (lwx + lwy))
    else:
        n_frame += 1
    T("E4 the senses in the pawl frame: the pair's Q* = sigma_x e eps_x (lam_x W_x - lam_y W_y) on every Mesh", pf == sx * AB * ex * (lwx - lwy))
    T("E4 the senses in the pawl frame keep Q* where the two ends' weights agree", pf == 0 or lwx != lwy)
    M("E4-m1 F1's senses keep Q* where the weights agree (fails on walls)", f1 == 0 or lwx != lwy)

print("c4, the one formula in exact rationals on a 3^3 torus")
T.report()
print("mutants (each must show failures):")
M.report()
bad = [k for k in M.order if M.fails(k) == 0]
print("every mutant failed" if not bad else f"MUTANTS THAT DID NOT FAIL: {bad}")
